\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\nu^{2}\|\tilde{m}_{v}\|_{\textup{in}}+\|m_{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}(\zeta_{*}\leq\zeta\leq\zeta^{*})}+\left\|Q_{\tilde{\nu}}-Q_{\nu}+P_{1,\tilde{\nu}}-P_{1,\nu}+P_{2,\nu}-N_{1,\tilde{\nu}}\right\|_{H^{2}(\zeta_{*}\leq\zeta\leq\zeta^{*})}\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}.

Using the explicit inversion formula of
\mathscr{A}_{0}^{-1}
, we write

\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}\tilde{m}_{v}=\frac{1}{2}\psi_{0}(r)\int_{r}^{1}\frac{\zeta^{4}+4\zeta^{2}\ln\zeta-1}{\zeta}f(\zeta)d\zeta+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{\psi}_{0}(r)\int_{0}^{r}\zeta f(\zeta)d\zeta+c_{0}\psi_{0},

where
\psi_{0}
and
\tilde{\psi}_{0}
are the two linearly independent solutions to
\mathscr{A}_{0}\psi=0
given by

\psi_{0}(r)=\frac{r^{2}}{\left<r\right>^{4}}\quad\textup{and}\quad\tilde{\psi}_{0}(r)=\frac{r^{4}+4r^{2}\ln r-1}{\left<r\right>^{4}}.

(139)

From the orthogonality condition (
96
), we use the coercivity of
\mathscr{A}_{0}
given in Lemma
B.1
to estimate the constant
|c_{0}|\lesssim\|\tilde{m}_{v}\|_{\textup{in}}\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}
. We then estimate by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and the decay of
\psi_{0}
,

\displaystyle k=0,1,\quad|(r\partial_{r})^{k}\tilde{m}_{v}(r)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|f\|_{L^{2}_{\omega_{0}}(r\leq\frac{\zeta^{*}}{2\nu})}\frac{r^{2}}{1+r^{2}}\sqrt{\ln\langle r\rangle}+\frac{|c_{0}|}{1+r^{2}}\lesssim\;\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}\frac{r^{2}}{1+r^{2}}\sqrt{\ln\langle r\rangle},

for
r\leq\zeta^{*}/2\nu
. This concludes the proof of the pointwise estimate (
134
).

Step 3
Radial far away pointwise estimates. We first prove (
136
). We recall the Sobolev embedding:

\||z|^{2-\frac{1}{4}}|\hat{w}^{0}|\|_{L^{\infty}(|z|\geq\frac{\zeta^{*}}{2})}\lesssim\|\hat{w}^{0}\|_{\textup{ex}}.

(140)

Due to the the bootstrap bound (
126
), the above inequality proves (
136
). From the relation
\partial_{\zeta}m_{\hat{w}}=\zeta\hat{w}^{0}
this in turn implies the inequality for
\zeta\geq\zeta^{*}/2
:

|\partial_{\zeta}m_{\hat{w}}|\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}\zeta^{\frac{1}{4}-1}.

From (
124
) and Sobolev embedding, we get

|m_{\hat{w}}(\frac{\zeta^{*}}{2})|\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}.

The two above inequalities and the fundamental Theorem of Calculus then imply the second inequality in (
136
). Recall that

w-U_{\nu}=\Psi_{\nu}+\varepsilon,\ \ m_{w}-Q_{\nu}=P_{\nu}+m_{\varepsilon}.

from which and the relation (
119
), the pointwise estimate (
70
) and the bound (
136
), we deduce (
135
).

Step 4
Estimate of the Poisson field. We first note that from (
130
) and (
128
), and the Sobolev embedding (
140
) there holds for
|z|\geq\zeta_{*}/2
:

|\varepsilon^{\perp}(z)|\lesssim e^{-\kappa\tau}|z|^{-2+\frac{1}{4}}.

(141)

From a change of variables, the coercivity given in Lemma
2.3
and the bootstrap bound (
127
), we have the localized estimate

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}|\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}q^{\perp}|^{2}\frac{dy}{U(y)}\lesssim\frac{e^{-2\kappa\tau}}{\nu^{2}}.

Using this estimate and (
137
) yield

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}|\varepsilon^{\perp}(z)|^{2}(1+|z|)^{2\alpha}dz\lesssim\frac{e^{-2\kappa\tau}}{\nu^{6}},

for any
\alpha<3/4
. Applying (
272
) for
\alpha=1/2
then implies (
138
).
∎

4. Control of the solution in the bootstrap regime

This section is devoted to the proof of Proposition
3.4
. It will be showed through a series of Lemmas in which the dynamics of the parameters is controlled and the a priori estimates for the remainder are bootstrapped.

4.1. Modulation equations

Injecting the decomposition (
88
) in the equation (
54
), we obtain the following equation of the remainder in the partial mass setting:

\partial_{\tau}m_{\varepsilon}=\mathscr{A}^{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon}+\frac{\partial_{\zeta}\big[(2P_{\nu}+m_{\varepsilon})m_{\varepsilon}\big]}{2\zeta}+m_{E}+N_{0}(\varepsilon^{\perp}),

(142)

where

P_{\nu}
and
m_{E}
are introduced in (
86
) and (
97
) respectively and

N_{0}(\varepsilon^{\perp})=-\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{S(0,\zeta)}\varepsilon^{\perp}\Big(\nabla\Phi_{\varepsilon^{\perp}}-\frac{x_{\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\Big)\cdot\vec{n}dS.

(143)

We write from (
52
) and the decomposition (
88
) the equation satisfied by
\varepsilon^{\bot}
:

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}\varepsilon^{\bot}=\mathscr{L}^{z}\varepsilon^{\bot}-\nabla\cdot\mathcal{G}(\varepsilon^{\perp})+\frac{x_{\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\cdot\nabla W+\frac{x_{\tau}}{\mu}.\nabla\varepsilon^{0}+N^{\perp}(\varepsilon^{\perp}),

(144)

where
\mathscr{L}^{z}
is the linearized operator defined in (
17
) and

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}(\varepsilon^{\perp})

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{\perp}\nabla\Phi_{\Psi_{\nu}+\varepsilon^{0}}+(\Psi_{\nu}+\varepsilon^{0})\nabla\Phi_{\varepsilon^{\perp}},

(145)

\displaystyle N^{\perp}(\varepsilon^{\perp})

\displaystyle=-\left[\nabla\cdot\left(\varepsilon^{\perp}\Big(\nabla\Phi_{\varepsilon^{\perp}}-\frac{x_{\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\Big)\right)\right]^{\perp}.

(146)

Projecting (
142
) on the directions generated the orthogonality conditions gives the time evolution of the parameters below. The solution of the following equations gives the desired blowup laws as explained in the strategy of the proof.

Lemma 4.1

(Modulation equations)
.

Let
w
be a solution in the bootstrap regime in the sense of Definition
3.3
. Then, the following estimates hold on
[\tau_{0},\tau^{*}]
:

\displaystyle 8\nu^{2}\left(\dfrac{\nu_{\tau}}{\nu}-\beta\right)+a_{1,\tau}-2\beta a_{1}(1+

\displaystyle\tilde{\alpha}_{0})+\dfrac{a_{1}}{\ln\nu}\dfrac{\nu_{\tau}}{\nu}+a_{1}\frac{\beta_{\tau}}{\beta}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\mathcal{D}(\tau)}{|\ln\nu|}\|m_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|^{3}}\right),

(147)

a_{1,\tau}-2\beta a_{1}\tilde{\alpha}_{1}+\dfrac{a_{1}}{\ln\nu}\dfrac{\nu_{\tau}}{\nu}+a_{1}\frac{\beta_{\tau}}{\beta}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\mathcal{D}(\tau)}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\|m_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|^{3}}\right),

(148)

and for
n\in{2,\cdots,N}
,

a_{n,\tau}-2\beta a_{n}(1-n+\tilde{\alpha}_{n})=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\mathcal{D}(\tau)}{|\ln\nu|}\|m_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\right),

(149)

where
\tilde{\alpha}_{n}
is given in Proposition
2.1
and

\mathcal{D}(\tau)=\left|\frac{\nu_{\tau}}{\nu}\right|+\left|\frac{\beta_{\tau}}{\beta}\right|.

Proof.

Taking the scalar product of (
142
) with
\phi_{n,\nu}
in
L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}
for
n=0,...,N
, using the orthogonality (
89
) and the self-adjointness of
\mathscr{A}^{\zeta}
yields the identity

\langle\partial_{\tau}m_{\varepsilon},\phi_{n,\nu}\rangle=\left\langle\frac{\partial_{\zeta}\big[(2P_{\nu}+m_{\varepsilon})m_{\varepsilon}\big]}{2\zeta}+m_{E}+N_{0}(\varepsilon^{\perp}),\phi_{n,\nu}\right\rangle,

(150)

where we write
\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle\equiv\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}
for simplificity and
\omega_{\nu}
is the weight introduced in (
25
).

We are going to estimate all terms appearing in the above equation.

- The time derivative term
. We start by differentiating the first orthogonality condition in (
89
) to get

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\tau}\langle m_{\varepsilon},\phi_{n,\nu}\rangle=\langle\partial_{\tau}m_{\varepsilon},\phi_{n,\nu}\rangle+\Big\langle m_{\varepsilon},\frac{\nu_{\tau}}{\nu}(\nu\partial_{\nu}\phi_{n,\nu}+\frac{\nu\partial_{\nu}\omega_{\nu}}{\omega_{\nu}}\phi_{n,\nu})+\frac{\beta_{\tau}}{\beta}(\beta\partial_{\beta}\phi_{n,\nu}+\frac{\beta\partial_{\beta}\omega_{\nu}}{\omega_{\nu}}\phi_{n,\nu})\Big\rangle,

where we have the algebraic identities

\frac{\nu\partial_{\nu}\omega_{\nu}}{\omega_{\nu}}=\frac{\frac{\nu^{4}}{2}(1+\frac{\zeta^{2}}{\nu^{2}})}{\nu^{4}(1+\frac{\zeta^{2}}{\nu^{2}})^{2}}=\frac{1}{(1+\frac{\zeta^{2}}{\nu^{2}})},\qquad\frac{\beta\partial_{\beta}\omega_{\nu}}{\omega_{\nu}}=-\frac{\beta\zeta^{2}}{2}.

Using Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, (
68
) or (
64
), (
65
) and (
69
) if
n=0,1
, the orthogonality condition (
89
), and (
59
) for
n\geq 2
, one obtains

\displaystyle\left|\langle m_{\varepsilon},\frac{\beta\zeta^{2}}{2}\phi_{n,\nu}\rangle\right|+\left|\langle m_{\varepsilon},\beta\partial_{\beta}\phi_{n,\nu}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\langle m_{\varepsilon},\nu\partial_{\nu}\phi_{n,\nu}\rangle\right|\lesssim\|m_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\times\left\{\begin{array}[]{cl}1&\mbox{ if }\;n=0,1,\\
|\ln\nu|&\mbox{ if }\;n\geq 2.\end{array}\right.

From (
68
), (
64
) and (
59
), and Cauchy-Schwarz, we estimate for
n\in\{0,\cdots,N\}
,

\left|\langle m_{\varepsilon},\frac{\nu\partial_{\nu}\omega_{\nu}}{\omega_{\nu}}\phi_{n,\nu}\rangle\right|\lesssim\|m_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}.

Collecting the above estimates yields

\left|\langle\partial_{\tau}m_{\varepsilon},\phi_{n,\nu}\rangle\right|\lesssim\mathcal{D}(\tau)\|m_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\times\left\{\begin{array}[]{cl}1&\mbox{ if }\;n=0,1,\\
|\ln\nu|&\mbox{ if }\;n\geq 2.\end{array}\right.

(151)

- The lower order linear term
. One first writes by using integration by parts

\displaystyle\left\langle\frac{\partial_{\zeta}(P_{\nu}m_{\varepsilon})}{\zeta},\phi_{n,\nu}\right\rangle

(152)

\displaystyle=-\Big\langle m_{\varepsilon},\frac{\zeta}{\omega_{\nu}}P_{\nu}\partial_{\zeta}((\phi_{n,\nu}-\phi_{0,\nu})\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta^{2}})\Big\rangle-\Big\langle m_{\varepsilon},\frac{\zeta}{\omega_{\nu}}P_{\nu}\partial_{\zeta}(\phi_{0,\nu}\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta^{2}})\Big\rangle.

Using (
132
), (
133
) and the degeneracy near the origin (
70
), one obtains the rough bound

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\zeta}{\omega_{\nu}}P_{\nu}\partial_{\zeta}\big((\phi_{n,\nu}-\phi_{0,\nu})\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta^{2}}\big)\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\langle\zeta\rangle^{C}\frac{r^{2}\ln\langle r\rangle}{\langle r\rangle^{6}},

which yields the estimates

\Big\|\frac{\zeta}{\omega_{\nu}}P_{\nu}\partial_{\zeta}\big((\phi_{n,\nu}-\phi_{0,\nu})\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta^{2}}\big)\Big\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}},

\left|\Big\langle m_{\varepsilon},\frac{\zeta}{\omega_{\nu}}P_{\nu}\partial_{\zeta}((\phi_{n,\nu}-\phi_{0,\nu})\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta^{2}})\Big\rangle\right|\lesssim\frac{\|m_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}.

As for the last term in (
152
), we write
\phi_{0,\nu}=\frac{1}{\nu^{2}}T_{0}(r)+\tilde{\phi}_{0,\nu}
. Using the pointwise estimate (
66
), we have the estimate

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\zeta}{\omega_{\nu}}P_{\nu}\partial_{\zeta}\big(\tilde{\phi}_{0,\nu}\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta^{2}}\big)\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\left(\frac{\zeta}{\nu+\zeta}\right)^{2}\frac{\ln\langle r\rangle}{(\nu+\zeta)^{2}},

from which and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\Big\langle m_{\varepsilon},\frac{\zeta}{\omega_{\nu}}P_{\nu}\partial_{\zeta}(\tilde{\phi}_{0,\nu}\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta^{2}})\Big\rangle\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\|m_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}.

(153)

To estimate the contribution coming from
T_{0}
, we use the algebraic identity
8T_{0}=r^{2}U
and write

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{\omega_{\nu}}\partial_{\zeta}\left(\frac{1}{\nu^{2}}T_{0}(r)\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta^{2}}\right)=-\frac{\beta\zeta^{2}}{8\omega_{\nu}}e^{-\frac{\beta\zeta^{2}}{2}},

(154)

and recall from (
132
) and (
133
) that
P_{\nu}=P_{1,\nu}+P_{2,\nu}
satisfies

|P_{\nu}(\zeta)|\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\frac{\ln\langle r\rangle}{\langle r\rangle^{2}}\langle\zeta\rangle^{C}.

(155)

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\left|\Big\langle m_{\varepsilon},\frac{\zeta}{\omega_{\nu}}P_{\nu}\partial_{\zeta}\left(\frac{1}{\nu^{2}}T_{0}(r)\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta^{2}}\right)\Big\rangle\right|=\left|\frac{\beta}{8}\int m_{\varepsilon}\zeta P_{\nu}e^{-\beta\zeta^{2}/2}d\zeta\right|\lesssim\|m_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\left(\int\zeta^{3}P_{\nu}^{2}e^{-\beta\zeta^{2}}\omega_{\nu}^{-1}d\zeta\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\|m_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\times\frac{K}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\left(\int r^{3}\frac{\langle\nu r\rangle^{2C}\ln^{2}\langle r\rangle}{\langle r\rangle^{4}}e^{-\beta\nu^{2}r^{2}/2}U(r)dr\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\times\|m_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}.

Injecting these estimates into (
152
) yields

\left|\left\langle\frac{\partial_{\zeta}(P_{\nu}m_{\varepsilon})}{\zeta},\phi_{n,\nu}\right\rangle\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\|m_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}.

(156)

- The error term
. From the expression (
98
), the orthogonality (
58
), we have

\langle m_{E},\phi_{n,\nu}\rangle=\|\phi_{n,\nu}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\hat{\omega}}{\zeta}}}^{2}\times\textup{Mod}_{n}+\langle\tilde{m}_{E},\phi_{n,\nu}\rangle+\left\langle\frac{\partial_{\zeta}P_{\nu}^{2}}{2\zeta},\phi_{n,\nu}\right\rangle,

(157)

where the contribution of
\langle\tilde{m}_{E},\phi_{n,\nu}\rangle
is precisely given by (
101
), (
102
) and (
103
). Applying (
156
) with
m_{\varepsilon}=P_{\nu}
, we have the estimate

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\frac{\partial_{\zeta}(P_{\nu}^{2})}{\zeta},\phi_{n,\nu}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\big(\|P_{1,\nu}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}+\|P_{2,\nu}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\big)\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\left(\nu^{2}|\ln\nu|+\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}\right)\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}.

As for the projection on
\phi_{0,\nu}
, we again use the relation
8T_{0}=r^{2}U
to obtain a better estimate. By integration by parts and
\phi_{0,\nu}=\frac{1}{\nu^{2}}T_{0}(r)+\tilde{\phi}_{0,\nu}
, we write

\left\langle\frac{\partial_{\zeta}(P_{\nu}^{2})}{2\zeta},\phi_{0,\nu}\right\rangle=\left\langle P_{\nu},\frac{\zeta}{\omega_{\nu}}P_{\nu}\partial_{\zeta}\Big((\frac{1}{\nu^{2}}T_{0}(r)+\tilde{\phi}_{0,\nu})\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{2\zeta^{2}}\Big)\right\rangle,

Applying (
153
) with
m_{\varepsilon}=P_{2,\nu}
yields

\left|\left\langle P_{2,\nu},\frac{\zeta}{\omega_{\nu}}P_{\nu}\partial_{\zeta}\Big(\tilde{\phi}_{0,\nu}\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta^{2}}\Big)\right\rangle\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\|P_{2,\nu}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|^{3}}.

A similar estimate for (
153
), by using
|P_{1,\nu}(\zeta)|\lesssim\nu^{2}\ln\langle r\rangle
, yields the estimate

\left|\left\langle P_{1,\nu},\frac{\zeta}{\omega_{\nu}}P_{\nu}\partial_{\zeta}\Big(\tilde{\phi}_{0,\nu}\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta^{2}}\Big)\right\rangle\right|\lesssim\nu^{4}|\ln\nu|^{C}\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|^{3}}.

From (
154
) and (
155
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle P_{\nu},\frac{\zeta}{\omega_{\nu}}P_{\nu}\partial_{\zeta}\Big(\frac{1}{\nu^{2}}T_{0}(r)\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta^{2}}\Big)\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{\beta}{8}\int P_{\nu}^{2}\zeta e^{-\beta\zeta^{2}/2}d\zeta\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|^{4}}\int\frac{\ln^{2}\langle r\rangle}{\langle r\rangle^{4}}\langle\nu r\rangle^{2C}re^{-\beta\nu^{2}r^{2}/2}dr\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|^{4}}.

We then conclude

\left|\left\langle\frac{\partial_{\zeta}P_{\nu}^{2}}{2\zeta},\phi_{n,\nu}\right\rangle\right|\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|},\quad\left|\left\langle\frac{\partial_{\zeta}P_{\nu}^{2}}{2\zeta},\phi_{0,\nu}\right\rangle\right|\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|^{3}}.

(158)

- The nonlinear term
. We write by integration by parts using
\phi_{0,\nu}=\frac{1}{\nu^{2}}T_{0}(r)+\tilde{\phi}_{0,\nu}
,

\displaystyle\Big\langle\frac{\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{2\zeta},\phi_{n,\nu}\Big\rangle

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\Big\langle m_{\varepsilon}^{2},\frac{\zeta}{\omega_{\nu}}\partial_{\zeta}\Big(\frac{(\phi_{n,\nu}-\phi_{0,\nu})}{\zeta^{2}}\omega_{\nu}\Big)\Big\rangle-\frac{1}{2}\Big\langle m_{\varepsilon}^{2},\frac{\zeta}{\omega_{\nu}}\partial_{\zeta}(\frac{\frac{1}{\nu^{2}}T_{0}(r)+\tilde{\phi}_{0,\nu}}{\zeta^{2}}\omega_{\nu})\Big\rangle.

From the degeneracy near the origin (
70
), we have the rough bound

\left|\frac{\zeta}{\omega_{\nu}}\partial_{\zeta}\Big(\frac{(\phi_{n,\nu}-\phi_{0,\nu})}{\zeta^{2}}\omega_{\nu}\Big)\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{\nu^{2}}\frac{\ln\langle r\rangle}{\langle r\rangle^{2}}\langle\zeta\rangle^{C},

and from the pointwise estimate (
66
),

\left|\frac{\zeta}{\omega_{\nu}}\partial_{\zeta}\Big(\frac{\tilde{\phi}_{0,\nu}}{\zeta^{2}}\omega_{\nu}\Big)\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{\nu^{2}}\frac{\ln\langle r\rangle}{\langle r\rangle^{2}}.

We directly get the bounds

\left|\Big\langle m_{\varepsilon}^{2},\frac{\zeta}{\omega_{\nu}}\partial_{\zeta}\Big(\frac{(\phi_{n,\nu}-\phi_{0,\nu})}{\zeta^{2}}\omega_{\nu}\Big)\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}(\zeta\leq\zeta_{*})}\right|+\left|\Big\langle m_{\varepsilon}^{2},\frac{\zeta}{\omega_{\nu}}\partial_{\zeta}\Big(\frac{\tilde{\phi}_{0,\nu}}{\zeta^{2}}\omega_{\nu}\Big)\Big\rangle\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{\nu^{2}}\|m_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}.

From the pointwise bound far away (
136
) and the definition of
P_{2,\nu}
, we have
|m_{\varepsilon}(\zeta)|\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}\langle\zeta\rangle^{C}
, from which we get

\left|\Big\langle m_{\varepsilon}^{2},\frac{\zeta}{\omega_{\nu}}\partial_{\zeta}\Big(\frac{(\phi_{n,\nu}-\phi_{0,\nu})}{\zeta^{2}}\omega_{\nu}\Big)\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}(\zeta\geq\zeta_{*})}\right|\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\int_{\zeta\geq\zeta_{*}}\zeta^{C}e^{-\beta\zeta^{2}/2}d\zeta\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}.

Using (
154
), we compute

\displaystyle\left|\Big\langle m_{\varepsilon}^{2},\frac{\zeta}{\omega_{\nu}}\partial_{\zeta}(\frac{T_{0}(r)}{\nu^{2}\zeta^{2}}\omega_{\nu})\Big\rangle\right|=\left|\frac{\beta}{8\nu^{4}}\int m_{\varepsilon}^{2}\zeta^{2}U(r)\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta\right|=\left|\frac{\beta\nu^{2}}{8}\int m_{q}^{2}r^{2}U(r)\frac{\omega}{r}dr\right|\lesssim\nu^{2}\|m_{q}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega}{r}}}=\frac{1}{\nu^{2}}\|m_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}.

Collecting the above estimates yields the final bound for the nonlinear term

\left|\Big\langle\frac{\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{2\zeta},\phi_{n,\nu}\Big\rangle\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{\nu^{2}}\|m_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}+\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\lesssim\frac{K^{2}\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}.

(159)

- The nonradial term
. We estimate from (
138
) and (
59
) and (
201
),

\left|\Big\langle N_{0}(\varepsilon^{\perp}),\phi_{n,\nu}\Big\rangle\right|\lesssim\frac{e^{-2\kappa\tau}}{\nu^{C}}\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}.

(160)

for some universal constant
C
depending only on
N
, where we used in the second identity the bootstrap estimate (
120
) and
\tau_{0}
sufficiently large.

The conclusion follows by injecting (
151
), (
156
), (
157
), (
158
), (
159
) and (
160
) into the expression (
150
) and using (
58
). This ends the proof of Lemma
4.1
.
∎

4.2. Main energy estimate

This subsection is devoted to derive an energy estimate for the norm (
123
) of
m_{\varepsilon}
in
L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}
. Taking into account the decomposition (
94
) and the smallness of the higher order part of the approximate perturbation
P_{2,\nu}
in
L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}
, i.e.
\|P_{2,\nu}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}
(from (
122
) and (
59
)), we will control instead of
m_{\varepsilon}
the full higher order part of the perturbation:

\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}=m_{\varepsilon}+P_{2,\nu}.

Recall from (
88
) and (
94
) the decomposition

m_{w}=Q_{\nu}+P_{1,\nu}+\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\quad\textup{and}\quad\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}=Q_{\tilde{\nu}}-Q_{\nu}+P_{1,\tilde{\nu}}-P_{1,\nu}-N_{1,\tilde{\nu}}+\tilde{m}_{w},

and write from (
54
) the equation satisfied by
\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}
,

\partial_{\tau}\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}=\bar{\mathscr{A}}^{\zeta}\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}+\frac{\partial_{\zeta}\big[(V+\tilde{m}_{w})\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\big]}{2\zeta}+\bar{m}_{E}+N_{0}(\varepsilon^{\perp}),

(161)

where
N_{0}(\varepsilon^{\perp})
is defined as in (
143
) and

V=P_{1,\nu}+P_{1,\tilde{\nu}}-N_{1,\tilde{\nu}}.

Here,
\bar{\mathscr{A}}^{\zeta}
is the modified operator defined by

\bar{\mathscr{A}}^{\zeta}=\mathscr{A}^{\zeta}+\frac{\partial_{\zeta}\big((Q_{\tilde{\nu}}-Q_{\nu})\cdot\big)}{2\zeta}=\frac{1}{2}\big(\mathscr{A}^{\zeta}+\tilde{\mathscr{A}}^{\zeta}\big),

(162)

where
\mathscr{A}^{\zeta}
and
\tilde{\mathscr{A}}^{\zeta}
are the linearized operator around
Q_{\nu}
and
Q_{\tilde{\nu}}
respectively, and the error
\bar{m}_{E}
is:

\bar{m}_{E}=\sum_{n=0}^{1}\textup{Mod}_{n}\;\phi_{n,\nu}(\zeta)+\tilde{\bar{m}}_{E}+\frac{\partial_{\zeta}P_{1,\nu}^{2}}{2\zeta},

(163)

(almost the same as (
98
) without taking into account the higher order approximate perturbation
P_{2,N}
) and the analogue of (
103
) holds, namely

\|\tilde{\bar{m}}_{E}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}+\left\|\frac{\partial_{\zeta}P_{1,\nu}^{2}}{2\zeta}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}\right).

(164)

The basic idea behind this modification is the ability of controlling the nonlinear term when performing the
L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}
energy estimate thanks to

1.

The pointwise bound (
134
) for
\tilde{m}_{w}
, which avoids the resonance
T_{0}
of the operator
\mathscr{L}_{0}
from the orthogonality condition (
96
). Recall that
T_{0}
is obtained by differentiating the rescaled stationary state
Q_{\nu}
at
\nu=1
, so it is natural to slightly modify the parameter function
\nu
by
\tilde{\nu}
to cancel out the component
T_{0}
. In particular, the orthogonality condition (
96
) allows us to derive the coercivity of
\mathscr{A}_{0}
in Lemma
B.2
, which is a key ingredient in obtaining the control of
\tilde{m}_{w}
as in (
125
).

2.

The spectral gap of
\bar{\mathscr{A}}^{\zeta}
still holds true under the orthogonality conditions (
89
) up to a sufficiently small error. The key feature is that this operator can be written as

\bar{\mathscr{A}}^{\zeta}=\mathscr{A}_{0}^{\zeta}+\mathcal{P}-\beta\zeta\partial_{\zeta}\quad\textup{with}\quad\mathcal{P}=\frac{1}{2\zeta}\partial_{\zeta}\big(\mathcal{V}\cdot\big),\quad\mathcal{V}=(Q_{\nu}-Q_{\tilde{\nu}})

where the particular form of the perturbation
\mathcal{P}
yields a cancellation as it is orthogonal to
T_{0}
in
L^{2}(\omega_{0}/\zeta)
. Roughly speaking, the modified eigenvalues and eigenfunctions are the same up to some sufficiently small error, which is enough to obtain for
\bar{\mathscr{A}}^{\zeta}
almost the same spectral gap as for
\mathscr{A}^{\zeta}
.

We first claim the following spectral properties of
\bar{\mathscr{A}}^{\zeta}
.

Lemma 4.2

(Spectral gap for
\bar{\mathscr{A}}^{\zeta}
)
.

There exists a universal
C^{\prime}>0
such that the following holds. Assume
1/2\leq\beta\leq 2
and
|\nu-\tilde{\nu}|\leq C\nu/|\log\nu|
for some
C>0
, and let
\bar{\omega}_{\nu}=\sqrt{\omega_{\nu}\omega_{\tilde{\nu}}}
. Fix any
N\in\mathbb{N}
and assume the orthogonality condition (
89
), i.e.
m_{\varepsilon}\perp\phi_{n,\nu}
in
L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}
for
0\leq n\leq N
. Then, for
\nu
small enough:

\int\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\bar{\mathscr{A}}^{\zeta}\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta\leq-2\beta(N-C^{\prime})\int\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}^{2}\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta+C\sum_{n=2}^{N}|a_{n}|^{2}|\ln\nu|^{2}.

(165)

Lemma
4.2
is a direct consequence of the following proposition whose proof is given in details in our previous work, and of the bound (
172
) on
\nu-\tilde{\nu}
whose proof is relegated at the end of this Subsection.

Proposition 4.3

(Spectral properties of
\bar{\mathscr{A}}^{\zeta}
,
[
10
]
)
.

Assume the hypotheses of Proposition
2.1
, and that the function
\mathcal{V}
in the operator
\mathcal{P}
satisfies

|\mathcal{V}(\zeta)|+|\zeta\partial_{\zeta}\mathcal{V}(\zeta)|\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{(\nu^{2}+\zeta^{2})^{2}},

Then, the operator
\bar{\mathscr{A}}^{\zeta}:H^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}\rightarrow L^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}
is essentially self-adjoint with compact resolvant, where

\bar{\omega}_{\nu}(\zeta)=\omega_{\nu}(\zeta)\exp\left(\int_{0}^{\zeta}\frac{\mathcal{P}(\tilde{\zeta})}{\tilde{\zeta}}d\tilde{\zeta}\right).

Its first
N+1
eigenvalues
(\bar{\alpha}_{n})_{0\leq n\leq N}
satisfy

|\bar{\alpha}_{n}-\alpha_{n}|\leq\frac{C^{\prime}}{|\log\nu|^{2}},

(166)

and there exist associated renormalised eigenfunctions
(\bar{\phi}_{n,\nu})_{0\leq n\leq N}
satisfying

\frac{\|\bar{\phi}_{n,\nu}-\phi_{n,\nu}\|_{L^{2}(\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta})}}{\|\phi_{n,\nu}\|_{L^{2}(\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta})}}\leq C^{\prime}\frac{\|\phi_{n,\nu}\|_{L^{2}(\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta})}}{\sqrt{|\log\nu|}}.

(167)

Remark 4.4
.

Using the fact that
\nu\sim\tilde{\nu}
(see (
172
)) and the explicit relation
\bar{\omega}_{\nu}=\left(\frac{\zeta^{2}+\tilde{\nu}^{2}}{\zeta^{2}+\nu^{2}}\right)\omega_{\nu}\sim\omega_{\nu}
, we have the following equivalence of weighted Lebesgue norms:

\|\cdot\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\sim\|\cdot\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}.

Proof of Lemma
4.2
.

The proof uses a standard orthogonal decomposition, and the spectral stability estimates provided by Proposition
4.3
. We recall that
\bar{\mathscr{A}}^{\zeta}
is self-adjoint in
L^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}
, with eigenvalues
\bar{\alpha}_{n}
and eigenfunctions
\bar{\phi}_{n,\nu}
. We decompose
m_{\varepsilon}
onto the first
N+1
eigenmodes of
\bar{\mathscr{A}}^{\zeta}
:

\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}=\sum_{n=0}^{N}b_{n}\bar{\phi}_{n,\nu}+\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}^{\perp},\ \ \bar{m}_{\varepsilon}^{\perp}\perp\bar{\phi}_{n}\mbox{ in }L^{2}(\bar{\omega}_{\nu}/\zeta)\mbox{ for }0\leq n\leq N,

(168)

so that from Proposition
4.3
and the spectral Theorem, there holds the spectral gap:

\int\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}^{\perp}\bar{\mathscr{A}}^{\zeta}\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}^{\perp}\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta\leq\bar{\alpha}_{N+1}\int\big|\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}^{\perp}\big|^{2}\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta\leq-2\beta(N-1)\int\big|\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}^{\perp}\big|^{2}\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta

where we used (
166
) and took
\nu
small enough in the last inequality. As the eigenfunctions
\bar{\phi}_{n,\nu}
are mutually orthogonal in
L^{2}(\bar{\omega}_{\nu}/\zeta)
, we have from the above inequality:

\displaystyle\int\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\bar{\mathscr{A}}^{\zeta}\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta

\displaystyle=\int\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}^{\perp}\bar{\mathscr{A}}^{\zeta}\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}^{\perp}\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta+\sum_{n=0}^{N}b_{n}^{2}\bar{\alpha}_{n}\int|\bar{\phi}_{n,\nu}|^{2}\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta

\displaystyle\quad\leq-2\beta(N-1)\int\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}^{2}\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta+\sum_{n=0}^{N}b_{n}^{2}|\alpha_{n}|\|\bar{\phi}_{n,\nu}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}.

Above the parameters
b_{n}
satisfy, from the orthogonality (
89
),
|\omega_{\nu}-\bar{\omega}_{\nu}|\lesssim\omega_{\nu}/|\ln\nu|
and (
167
):

\displaystyle b_{n}\|\bar{\phi}_{n,\nu}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}^{2}=\int\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\bar{\phi}_{n,\nu}\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta

\displaystyle\quad=\int\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}(\bar{\phi}_{n,\nu}-\phi_{n,\nu})\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta+\int\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\phi_{n,\nu}\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}-\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta+a_{n}\|\phi_{n,\nu}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\mathbf{1}_{\{2\leq n\leq N\}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\|\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\left(\|\bar{\phi}_{n,\nu}-\phi_{n,\nu}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}+\left\|\phi_{n,\nu}\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}-\omega_{\nu}}{\omega_{\nu}}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\right)+|a_{n}|\|\phi_{n,\nu}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\mathbf{1}_{\{2\leq n\leq N\}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\frac{\|\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\|\phi_{n,\nu}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}}{|\ln\nu|}+|a_{n}|\|\phi_{n,\nu}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\mathbf{1}_{\{2\leq n\leq N\}}.

Estimate (
165
) for
\nu
small enough then follows from the two above inequalities and (
167
). This concludes the proof of Lemma
4.2
assuming Proposition
4.3
.
∎

We are now in the position to derive the main energy decay of
\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}
.

Lemma 4.5

(Monotonicity of
\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}
in
L^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}
)
.

Let
w
be a solution in the bootstrap regime in the sense of Definition
3.3
. Then, for
\tau_{0}
large enough the following estimate holds on
[\tau_{0},\tau^{*}]
:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{d\tau}\big\|\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}

\displaystyle\leq-2\beta\left(N-C\right)\big\|\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}+C\big\|\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\big\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\left(\frac{|\textup{Mod}_{0}|}{\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}}+|\textup{Mod}_{1}|\right)+C\left(\sum_{n=2}^{N}|a_{n}|^{2}|\ln\nu|^{2}+\frac{\nu^{4}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\right),

(169)

where
C>0
is a universal constant independent on the bootstrap constants
N
,
\kappa
,
K
,
K^{\prime}
and
K^{\prime\prime}
, and
\textup{Mod}_{0}
and
\textup{Mod}_{1}
are given as in (
99
) and (
100
).

Proof.

We multiply the equation (
161
) with
\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}
and integrate over
[0,+\infty)
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{d\tau}\int\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}^{2}\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}^{2}\frac{\partial_{\tau}\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta+\int\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\bar{\mathscr{A}}^{\zeta}\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta+\int\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\bar{m}_{E}\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta

\displaystyle+\int\frac{\partial_{\zeta}(V+\tilde{m}_{w})\bar{m}_{\varepsilon})}{2\zeta}\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta+\int N_{0}(\varepsilon^{\perp})\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta.

In the following, we shall write
\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle
for
\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle_{L^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}
for simplicity.

- The time derivative term.
We first compute

\displaystyle\frac{\partial_{\tau}\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}=\partial_{\tau}\ln\bar{\omega}_{\nu}=\frac{1}{2}\frac{\nu_{\tau}}{\nu}\frac{1}{1+r^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{\tilde{\nu}_{\tau}}{\tilde{\nu}}\frac{1}{1+\tilde{r}^{2}}-\beta_{\tau}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{2}.

We obviously have the bound

\left|\Big\langle\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}^{2},\frac{\nu_{\tau}}{\nu}\frac{1}{1+r^{2}}+\frac{\tilde{\nu}_{\tau}}{\tilde{\nu}}\frac{1}{1+\tilde{r}^{2}}\Big\rangle\right|\leq\left(\left|\frac{\nu_{\tau}}{\nu}\right|+\left|\frac{\tilde{\nu}_{\tau}}{\tilde{\nu}}\right|\right)\|\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}},

and

\quad\left|\Big\langle\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}^{2},-\beta_{\tau}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{2}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}(\zeta\leq 1)}\right|\leq|\beta_{\tau}|\|\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}.

For
\zeta>1
, we use (
136
) to get

|\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}(\zeta)|\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}\langle\zeta\rangle^{C}\quad\textup{for}\quad\zeta\geq 1,

(170)

from which we obtain

\left|\Big\langle\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}^{2},\beta_{\tau}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{2}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}(\zeta\geq 1)}\right|\lesssim|\beta_{\tau}|\frac{\nu^{4}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\zeta^{C}e^{-\frac{\beta\zeta^{2}}{2}}d\zeta\lesssim\frac{\nu^{4}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}.

Hence,

\left|\int\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}^{2}\frac{\partial_{\tau}\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta\right|\lesssim\left(\left|\frac{\nu_{\tau}}{\nu}\right|+\left|\frac{\tilde{\nu}_{\tau}}{\tilde{\nu}}\right|+|\beta_{\tau}|\right)\|\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}+\frac{\nu^{4}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}.

(171)

- The linear and error terms.
We have by Lemma
4.2
,

\big\langle\bar{\mathscr{A}}^{\zeta}\bar{m}_{\varepsilon},\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\big\rangle\leq-\beta(N-C^{\prime})\|\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}+C\sum_{n=2}^{N}|a_{n}|^{2}|\ln\nu|^{2}.

From the decomposition (
163
) and the fact that
\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\perp\phi_{n,\nu}
for
n=0,1
in
L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}
, we write

\displaystyle\big\langle\bar{m}_{\varepsilon},\bar{m}_{E}\big\rangle=\sum_{n=0}^{1}\textup{Mod}_{n}\Big\langle\bar{m}_{\varepsilon},\phi_{n,\nu}\frac{\omega_{\tilde{\nu}}-\omega_{\nu}}{\omega_{\tilde{\nu}}}\Big\rangle+\big\langle\bar{m}_{\varepsilon},\tilde{\bar{m}}_{E}+\frac{P_{1}\partial_{\zeta}P_{1}}{\zeta}\big\rangle.

From Lemma
4.1
and (
164
), we have by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\left|\big\langle\bar{m}_{\varepsilon},\bar{m}_{E}\big\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\left(\sum_{n=0}^{1}\left|\textup{Mod}_{n}\right|\left\|\phi_{n,\nu}\frac{\omega_{\tilde{\nu}}-\omega_{\nu}}{\omega_{\tilde{\nu}}}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}+\left\|\tilde{\bar{m}}_{E}+\frac{P_{1}\partial_{\zeta}P_{1}}{\zeta}\right\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\|\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\left(\frac{|\textup{Mod}_{0}|}{\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}}+|\textup{Mod}_{1}|+\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}\right).

- The small linear term.
From (
132
), we have the bound

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\partial_{\zeta}V}{2\zeta}\right|+\left|\frac{\zeta}{2\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}\partial_{\zeta}\Big(\frac{V}{2\zeta^{2}}\bar{\omega}_{\nu}\Big)\right|\leq C\left(\left|\frac{\partial_{\zeta}V}{\zeta}\right|+\left|\frac{V}{\zeta^{2}}\right|\right)\leq C\frac{\ln^{2}\langle r\rangle}{1+r^{2}}\leq C,

from which and from an integration by parts, we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\Big\langle\frac{\partial_{\zeta}\big(V\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\big)}{2\zeta},\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\right|\leq C\int\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}^{2}\left(\left|\frac{\partial_{\zeta}V}{\zeta}\right|+\left|\frac{\zeta}{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}\partial_{\zeta}\left(\frac{V\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}\right)\right|\right)\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta\leq C\|\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}.

- The nonlinear term.
We use integration by part and the pointwise estimates (
134
) and (
135
) to get

\displaystyle\left|\Big\langle\frac{\partial_{\zeta}\big(\tilde{m}_{w}\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\big)}{2\zeta},\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\right|=\int\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}^{2}\left(\left|\frac{\partial_{\zeta}(\tilde{m}_{w})}{2\zeta}\right|+\left|\frac{\zeta}{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}\partial_{\zeta}\left(\frac{(\tilde{m}_{w})\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}\right)\right|\right)\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|}\int\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}^{2}\frac{\nu^{2}}{\zeta^{2}+\nu^{2}}\sqrt{\ln\langle r\rangle}\langle\zeta\rangle^{C}\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|}\int_{\zeta\leq 1}\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}^{2}\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta+\frac{\nu^{6}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\int_{\zeta\geq 1}\langle\zeta\rangle^{3C}e^{-\beta\zeta^{2}/2}d\zeta

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|}\|\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}+\frac{\nu^{6}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}.

- The nonradial term
. Since the contribution from the nonradial term is exponentially small in
\tau
, we just need a rough estimate by splitting the integration into two parts
\zeta\leq\zeta_{*}/2
and
\zeta\leq\zeta_{*}/2
, then using the pointwise estimates (
138
), (
134
), (
135
) and (
201
) to get

\left|\Big\langle N_{0}(\varepsilon^{\perp}),m_{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle\right|\lesssim e^{-\kappa\tau}\lesssim\frac{\nu^{4}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}.

The collection of the above estimates and the fact that
\Big|\frac{\nu_{\tau}}{\nu}\Big|+\Big|\frac{\tilde{\nu}_{\tau}}{\tilde{\nu}}\Big|+|\beta_{\tau}|\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|}
from (
147
), (
148
), (
119
) and (
172
) yield the conclusion of Lemma
4.5
.
∎

In view of the monotonicity formula (
169
), we need to estimate
\tilde{\nu}
and
\tilde{\nu}_{\tau}
in term of
\nu
. It is a consequence of the orthogonality condition (
89
) and of the rough estimate (
123
).

Lemma 4.6

(Estimate for
\tilde{\nu}
)
.

We have

|\tilde{\nu}^{2}-\nu^{2}|+|(\tilde{\nu}^{2}-\nu^{2})_{\tau}|\lesssim\frac{K}{\sqrt{\ln M}}\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}.

(172)

Proof.

We recall from the decomposition (
94
),

\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}=Q_{\tilde{\nu}}-Q_{\nu}+P_{1,\tilde{\nu}}-P_{1,\nu}-N_{1,\tilde{\nu}}+\tilde{m}_{w},

subject to the orthogonality conditions from (
89
) and (
96
),

\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\perp_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\phi_{0,\nu},\phi_{1,\nu},\quad\int\tilde{m}_{w}(\tilde{\nu}\tilde{r})\chi_{{}_{M}}(\tilde{r})T_{0}(\tilde{r})\frac{\omega_{0}(\tilde{r})}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}=0.

Recall that
8T_{0}(r)=r^{2}U(r)
, so the last condition is then written as

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle=\int\tilde{m}_{w}(\nu r)\chi_{{}_{M}}(r)rdr=\int\Big[\bar{m}_{q}(r)+Q(r)-Q(rh)+P_{1}(r)-P_{1}(rh)+N_{1}(rh)\Big]\chi_{{}_{M}}(r)rdr,

where
\bar{m}_{q}(r)=\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}(\nu r)
satisfies the estimate
\|\bar{m}_{q}\|_{L^{2}_{\omega/r}}\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|}
,
P_{1}(r)=P_{1,\nu}(\nu r)
, and we write for short

h=\frac{\nu}{\tilde{\nu}}.

A direct calculation yields

\displaystyle\int\big(Q(r)-Q(rh)\big)\chi_{{}_{M}}(r)rdr

\displaystyle=(1-h^{2})\int\frac{r^{2}}{(1+h^{2}r^{2})(1+r^{2})}\chi_{{}_{M}}(r)rdr=(1-h^{2})|\ln M|\big(1+o_{M\to+\infty}(1)\big).

From Lemma
2.2
and the asymptotic behavior of
T_{1}
given in (
33
), we estimate for
r\leq 2M
,

\displaystyle\big|P_{1}(r)-P_{1}(rh)\big|

\displaystyle=\left|2\beta a_{1}\big(T_{1}(r)-T_{1}(rh)\Big)\right|+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{|a_{1}|}{|\ln\nu|}\right)\lesssim\nu^{2}\left(|\ln h|+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\ln^{2}r}{r^{2}}\right)+\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|}\right),

which gives the estimate

\left|\int\Big(P_{1}(r)-P_{1}(rh)\Big)\chi_{{}_{M}}(r)rdr\right|\lesssim\left(|\ln h|+\nu^{2}|\ln\nu|^{3}+\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|}\right)\lesssim|1-h^{2}|+\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|}.

We have by Cauchy-Schwarz,

\left|\int\bar{m}_{q}\chi_{{}_{M}}(r)rdr\right|\lesssim\|\bar{m}_{q}\|_{L^{2}_{\omega/r}}\left(\int_{0}^{2M}\frac{r^{3}dr}{(1+r^{2})^{2}}dr\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\frac{K\sqrt{|\ln M|}}{|\ln\nu|}.

As for the correction term, we have the estimate

\displaystyle\left|\int N_{1}(rh)\chi_{M}rdr\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\int\mathscr{A}_{0}^{-1}\left(\frac{\partial_{r}P_{1}^{2}(rh)}{r}+8\beta\tilde{\nu}^{2}\tilde{\phi}_{0}(rh)\right)\chi_{M}rdr\right|\lesssim\tilde{\nu}^{4}\int_{r\leq 2M}r\ln\langle r\rangle dr\lesssim\tilde{\nu}^{4}M^{2}\ln M.

Gathering these estimates yields
|1-h^{2}|\lesssim\frac{K}{\sqrt{|\ln M|}|\ln\nu|}
, which implies the first estimate in (
172
).
The estimate for the time derivative is similar by using the identity

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle=\partial_{\tau}\int\tilde{m}_{w}(\nu r)\chi_{{}_{M}}(r)rdr=\partial_{\tau}\int\Big[\bar{m}_{q}(r)+Q(r)-Q(rh)+P_{1}(r)-P_{1}(rh)+N_{1}(rh)\Big)\Big]\chi_{{}_{M}}(r)rdr.

and the equation satisfied by
\bar{m}_{q}
, so we omit it here. This concludes the proof of Lemma
4.6
.
∎

4.3. Estimates at higher order regularity in the middle range

We are now using standard parabolic regularity techniques to derive the
H^{2}
control of
m_{\varepsilon}
in the middle range
\zeta_{*}\leq\zeta\leq\zeta^{*}
. In particular, we claim the following.

Lemma 4.7

(
H^{2}
control of
m_{\varepsilon}
in the middle range)
.

Let
w
be a solution in the bootstrap regime in the sense of Definition
3.3
. Then, we have the following bounds for
\tau\in[\tau_{0},\tau^{*}]
:

\|m_{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}(\zeta_{*}\leq\zeta\leq\zeta^{*})}\leq C(K,\zeta_{*},\zeta^{*})\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}.

(173)

Proof.

From the
L^{2}_{\frac{\hat{\omega}}{\zeta}}
control of
m_{\varepsilon}
, we already have the estimate

\|m_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}(\frac{\zeta_{*}}{4}\leq\zeta\leq 4\zeta^{*})}\leq C\|m_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\leq CK\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}\quad\textup{for some}\quad C=C(\zeta_{*},\zeta^{*})>0,

where
\tilde{\omega}(\zeta)=\zeta^{3}e^{-\beta\zeta^{2}/2}
.

We shall rely on this estimate to derive bounds for the higher derivatives. This regularity procedure is standard, but we give it for the sake of completeness. Let us consider
k=0,1,2
and
\chi_{k}(\zeta)
be a smooth cut-off function defined as

0\leq\chi_{k}(\zeta)\leq 1,\quad\chi_{k}(\zeta)=\left\{\begin{array}[]{cl}1&\quad\textup{for}\quad(k+2)\zeta^{*}/4\leq\zeta\leq(6-k)\zeta^{*}/4,\\
0&\quad\textup{for}\quad\zeta\in(0,(k+2)\zeta_{*}/8)\cap((6-k)\zeta^{*}/2,+\infty).\end{array}\right.

We also write for simplicity,

m_{\varepsilon,k}(\zeta,\tau)=\partial_{\zeta}^{k}m_{\varepsilon}(\zeta,\tau)\chi_{k}(\zeta)\quad\textup{for}\quad k=0,1,2.

From equation (
142
), we see that
m_{\varepsilon,k}
satisfy the equations

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}m_{\varepsilon,0}

\displaystyle=\mathscr{A}^{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon,0}+[\chi_{0},\mathscr{A}^{\zeta}]m_{\varepsilon}+\mathcal{F}\chi_{0},

(174)

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}m_{\varepsilon,1}

\displaystyle=\mathscr{A}^{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon,1}+[\chi_{1},\mathscr{A}^{\zeta}]\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon}+\big[\partial_{\zeta},\mathscr{A}^{\zeta}\big]m_{\varepsilon}\chi_{1}+\partial_{\zeta}\mathcal{F}\chi_{1},

(175)

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}m_{\varepsilon,2}=\mathscr{A}^{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon,2}

\displaystyle+[\chi_{2},\mathscr{A}^{\zeta}]\partial_{\zeta}^{2}m_{\varepsilon}+\big[\partial_{\zeta},\mathscr{A}^{\zeta}\big]\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon}\chi_{2}+\partial_{\zeta}\big[\partial_{\zeta},\mathscr{A}^{\zeta}\big]m_{\varepsilon}\chi_{2}+\partial_{\zeta}^{2}\mathcal{F}\chi_{2},

where
\mathscr{A}^{\zeta}
, introduced in (
19
), is rewritten as

\mathscr{A}^{\zeta}=\frac{1}{\tilde{\omega}}\partial_{\zeta}\Big(\tilde{\omega}\partial_{\zeta}\Big)+\mathcal{P}_{0}\quad\textup{with}\quad\mathcal{P}_{0}=-\frac{4\nu^{2}}{\zeta(\nu^{2}+\zeta^{2})}\partial_{\zeta}+U_{\nu},\quad\tilde{\omega}(\zeta)=\zeta^{3}e^{-\frac{\beta\zeta^{2}}{2}}.

The commutators are defined as

\displaystyle\big[\chi_{k},\mathscr{A}^{\zeta}\big]=-2\chi_{k}^{\prime}\partial_{\zeta}-\chi_{k}^{\prime\prime}+\left(\frac{1}{\zeta}-\frac{Q_{\nu}}{\zeta}+\beta\zeta\right)\chi_{k}^{\prime},

(176)

\displaystyle\big[\partial_{\zeta},\mathscr{A}^{\zeta}\big]=-\partial_{\zeta}\left(\frac{1-Q_{\nu}+\beta\zeta^{2}}{\zeta}\right)\partial_{\zeta}+\partial_{\zeta}U_{\nu},

(177)

and the source term is given by

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}=\frac{\partial_{\zeta}\big[(2P_{\nu}+m_{\varepsilon})m_{\varepsilon}\big]}{2\zeta}+m_{E}+N_{0}(\varepsilon^{\perp}).

From the second estimate in (
104
) and the bootstrap estimates in Definition
3.3
, we arrive at the following bound:

\big\|\mathcal{F}\chi_{0}\big\|_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}\leq C\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}.

(178)

Integrating (
174
) against
m_{\varepsilon,0}\tilde{\omega}
yields the energy identity

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{d\tau}\|m_{\varepsilon,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}

\displaystyle=-\|\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}+\frac{\beta_{\tau}}{2}\|m_{\varepsilon,0}\zeta\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}+\int_{0}^{+\infty}\Big(\mathcal{P}m_{\varepsilon,0}+[\chi_{0},\mathscr{A}^{\zeta}]m_{\varepsilon}+\mathcal{F}\chi_{0}\Big)m_{\varepsilon,0}\tilde{\omega}d\zeta.

Using the fact that
|\beta_{\tau}\zeta|\lesssim\frac{\zeta^{*}}{|\ln\nu|^{3}}
,
\left|\frac{\nu^{2}}{\zeta(\zeta^{2}+\nu^{2})}\right|+|U_{\nu}(\zeta)|\lesssim\nu^{2}
for
\zeta_{*}/4\leq\zeta\leq 4\zeta^{*}
and Young inequality yields

\displaystyle\frac{|\beta_{\tau}|}{2}\|m_{\varepsilon,0}\zeta\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}+\left|\int_{0}^{+\infty}\mathcal{P}_{0}m_{\varepsilon,0}m_{\varepsilon,0}\tilde{\omega}d\zeta\right|+\left|\int_{0}^{+\infty}\mathcal{F}\chi_{0}m_{\varepsilon,0}\tilde{\omega}d\zeta\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\nu^{2}\|\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon,0}\zeta\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}+\|m_{\varepsilon}\chi_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}+\|\mathcal{F}\chi_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}\lesssim\nu^{2}\|\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}+\frac{CK\nu^{4}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}.

Using integration by parts and Cauchy Schwarz inequality with
\epsilon
, we have

\left|\int_{0}^{+\infty}[\chi_{0},\mathscr{A}^{\zeta}]m_{\varepsilon}m_{\varepsilon,0}\tilde{\omega}d\zeta\right|\leq\frac{1}{4}\|\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon,0}\|_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}^{2}+C\|m_{\varepsilon}\chi_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}.

Gathering these above estimate yields

\frac{d}{d\tau}\|m_{\varepsilon,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}\leq-\|\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}+\frac{CK\nu^{4}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}.

(179)

Similarly, we integrate equations (
175
) and (
4.3
) against
m_{\varepsilon,1}\tilde{\omega}
and
m_{\varepsilon,2}\tilde{\omega}
respectively, then use integration by parts and Cauchy-Schwarz inequality with
\epsilon
and note that
\|m_{\varepsilon,1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}\leq\|\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}+\frac{CK\nu^{4}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}
and
\|m_{\varepsilon,2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}\leq\|\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon,1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}+\frac{CK\nu^{4}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}
by definition, to derive the following energy identities

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\tau}\|m_{\varepsilon,1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}

\displaystyle\leq-\|\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon,1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}+C_{1}\|\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}+\frac{CK\nu^{4}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}},

(180)

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\tau}\|m_{\varepsilon,2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}

\displaystyle\leq-\|\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon,2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}+C_{2}\|\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon,1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}+\frac{CK\nu^{4}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}.

(181)

By summing up (
179
), (
180
) and (
181
), we arrive at

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\tau}\left(\|m_{\varepsilon,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}+\frac{1}{2C_{1}}\|m_{\varepsilon,1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}+\frac{1}{4C_{1}C_{2}}\|m_{\varepsilon,2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq-\frac{1}{2}\|\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon,0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}-\frac{1}{4C_{1}}\|\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon,1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}-\frac{1}{4C_{1}C_{2}}\|\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon,2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\tilde{\omega}}}+\frac{CK\nu^{4}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}.

Using the above differential inequality and Poincaré inequality, we integrate in time to obtain the desire conclusion. This ends the proof of Lemma
4.7
.
∎

Lemma 4.8

(
H^{2}
control of
\varepsilon^{\perp}
in the middle range)
.

Let
w
be a solution in the bootstrap regime in the sense of Definition
3.3
. Then, we have the following bounds for
\tau\in[\tau_{0},\tau^{*}]
:

\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{H^{2}(\zeta_{*}\leq\zeta\leq\zeta^{*})}\leq C(K,\zeta_{*},\zeta^{*})e^{-\kappa\tau}.

(182)

Proof.

From the bootstrap bound (
127
) of
\varepsilon^{\perp}
and the equivalence of the norm (
107
), we already have the estimate

\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{L^{2}(\frac{\zeta_{*}}{4}\leq\zeta\leq 4\zeta^{*})}\leq C\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{0}\leq CKe^{-\kappa\tau}\quad\textup{for some}\quad C=C(\zeta_{*},\zeta^{*})>0.

from which and a standard parabolic regularity argument as for the proof of Lemma
4.7
yields the conclusion of Lemma
4.8
.
∎

4.4. Higher order regularity energy estimates in the inner zone

This section is devoted to the control of
\|\tilde{m}_{w}\|_{\textup{in}}
. The basic idea is that the scaling term
\beta\zeta\partial_{\zeta}\tilde{m}_{w}
is regarded as a small perturbation of
\mathscr{A}^{\zeta}
in the blowup zone
\zeta\leq\zeta_{*}
for some fixed small
\zeta_{*}\ll 1
, namely that the dynamics resembles
\partial_{\tau}\tilde{m}_{w}=\mathscr{A}_{0}^{\zeta}\tilde{m}_{w}
. Since
T_{0}
spans the kernel of
\mathscr{A}_{0}
, we need to rule it out by imposing the local orthogonality condition (
96
). It allows us to use the key ingredient, the coercivity of
\mathscr{A}_{0}
(see Lemma
B.1
below), for establishing the monotonicity formula of
\|\tilde{m}_{w}\|_{\textup{in}}
.

From the equation (
83
) and the decomposition (
91
),
\tilde{m}_{v}
satisfies the equation

\partial_{s}\tilde{m}_{v}=\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}+\tilde{\eta}\;\tilde{r}\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}+\frac{\partial_{\tilde{r}}\big[(\tilde{P}_{1}-\tilde{N}_{1})\tilde{m}_{v}\big]}{\tilde{r}}+\frac{\partial_{\tilde{r}}\big(\tilde{m}_{v}^{2}\big)}{2\tilde{r}}+\mathcal{E}_{1}(\tilde{r},s),

(183)

where
\mathscr{A}_{0}
is defined as in (
23
) but here acting on the variable
\tilde{r}
instead of
r
(the reader should bear in mind that
\tilde{m}_{v}
is a function of the
\tilde{r}
variable), and we write for simplicity

\tilde{\eta}=\frac{\tilde{\nu}_{s}}{\tilde{\nu}}-\beta\tilde{\nu}^{2}=\mathcal{O}(\nu^{2}),

and the error is given by

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1}

\displaystyle=\big(-\partial_{s}+\mathscr{A}_{0}\big)\big(\tilde{P}_{1}-\tilde{N}_{1}\big)+\tilde{\eta}\;\tilde{r}\partial_{\tilde{r}}\Big(Q+\tilde{P}_{1}-\tilde{N}_{1}\Big)+\frac{\partial_{\tilde{r}}(\tilde{P}_{1}-\tilde{N}_{1})^{2}}{2\tilde{r}}+\tilde{N}_{0}(v^{\perp}).

(184)

For a fixed small constant
\zeta_{*}
with
0<\zeta_{*}\ll 1
, we introduce

\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}(\tilde{r},s)=\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}(\tilde{r})\tilde{m}_{v}(\tilde{r},s),

(185)

where
\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}
is defined as in (
15
). We write from (
183
) the equation for
\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}
,

\partial_{s}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}=\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}+\tilde{\eta}\;\tilde{r}\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}+\frac{\partial_{\tilde{r}}\big((\tilde{P}_{1}-\tilde{N}_{1})\tilde{m}^{*}_{v}\big)}{\tilde{r}}+\frac{\partial_{\tilde{r}}\big(\tilde{m}^{*}_{v}\tilde{m}_{v}\big)}{2\tilde{r}}+\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\mathcal{E}_{1}+T(\tilde{m}_{v}),

where

\displaystyle T(\tilde{m}_{v})

\displaystyle=-\Big(\partial_{\tilde{r}}^{2}+\partial_{s}\big)\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\tilde{m}_{v}+\partial_{\tilde{r}}\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\left[-2\partial_{\tilde{r}}+\frac{1}{\tilde{r}}-\frac{\big[2Q+2\tilde{P}_{1}-2\tilde{N}_{1}+\tilde{m}_{v}\big]}{2\tilde{r}}+\tilde{\eta}\;\tilde{r}\right]\tilde{m}_{v}.

(186)

Thanks to the orthogonality condition (
96
), we can use the coercivity estimate (see Lemma
B.1
)

-\int\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\geq\delta_{1}\int\left(|\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}|^{2}+\frac{|\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}|^{2}}{1+\tilde{r}^{2}}\right)\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r},

(187)

and (see Lemma
B.2
)

\int|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}|^{2}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\geq\delta_{0}\int\left(|\partial_{\tilde{r}}^{2}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}|^{2}+\frac{|\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}|^{2}}{\langle\tilde{r}\rangle^{2}}+\frac{|\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}|^{2}}{\langle\tilde{r}\rangle^{4}(1+\ln^{2}\langle\tilde{r}\rangle)}\right)\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}.

(188)

Since the support of
\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}
is
\tilde{r}\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}
, we have by (
188
) the control

\int|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}|^{2}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\geq\frac{C\tilde{\nu}^{2}}{\zeta_{*}^{2}}\left(-\int\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\right).

(189)

Thanks to these coercivity estimates, we are able to establish the following monotonicity formula to control the inner norm.

Lemma 4.9

(Inner energy estimate)
.

We have for
\tau_{0}
large enough

\frac{d}{d\tau}\|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}}^{2}\leq C\left(\|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\zeta_{*}^{2}}\|m_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}(\zeta_{*}\leq\zeta\leq\zeta^{*})}+\frac{\nu^{4}}{\zeta_{*}^{2}|\ln\nu|^{2}}\right),

(190)

and

\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{d\tau}\left[-\tilde{\nu}^{2}\int\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}\right]\leq-\frac{1}{2}\|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}}+\frac{C^{\prime}}{\zeta_{*}^{2}}\|m_{\varepsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{2}(\zeta_{*}\leq\zeta\leq\zeta^{*})}+\frac{C^{\prime}\nu^{4}}{\zeta_{*}^{2}|\ln\nu|^{2}},

(191)

where
C
and
C^{\prime}
are independent from the constants
\kappa,K,K^{\prime},K^{\prime\prime}
and
\zeta_{*}
.

Proof.

Step 1

Energy estimate for the second order derivative
. We first prove (
190
). We integrate equation (
183
) against
\mathscr{A}_{0}^{2}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}
in
L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}
, and using the self-adjointness of
\mathscr{A}_{0}
in
L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}
and the fact that
\int f\mathscr{A}_{0}f\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\leq 0
(note that
T_{0}
is in the kernel of
\mathscr{A}_{0}
which is strictly positive on
(0,+\infty)
, so a standard Sturm-Liouville argument yields the non-negativity of
-\mathscr{A}_{0}
) to write the energy identity

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{ds}\int|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}|^{2}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\leq\int\mathscr{A}_{0}\big[\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\mathcal{E}_{1}+\mathcal{F}(\tilde{m}_{v})+T(\tilde{m}_{v})\big]\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r},

(192)

where

\mathcal{F}(\tilde{m}_{v})=\tilde{\eta}\;\tilde{r}\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}+\frac{\partial_{\tilde{r}}\big((\tilde{P}_{1}-\tilde{N}_{1})\tilde{m}^{*}_{v}\big)}{\tilde{r}}+\frac{\partial_{\tilde{r}}\big(\tilde{m}^{*}_{v}\tilde{m}_{v}\big)}{2\tilde{r}}.

The error term:
We claim the following estimate for the error term

\int\left|\mathscr{A}_{0}\big(\mathcal{E}_{1}(\tilde{r},s)\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}(\tilde{r})\big)\right|^{2}\frac{\omega_{0}(\tilde{r})}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\leq C\frac{\nu^{8}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}},

(193)

for some positive constant
C
which is independent from
K
,
K^{\prime}
and
K^{\prime\prime}
. Since the proof of (
193
) is technical and a bit lengthy, we postpone it to the end of this section and continue the proof of the lemma. We have by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, and

\displaystyle\left|\int\mathscr{A}_{0}\big(\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\mathcal{E}_{1}\big)\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\right|\lesssim\frac{\nu^{4}}{|\ln\nu|}\|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}}\lesssim\nu^{2}\|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}}^{2}+\frac{\nu^{6}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}.

The scaling term:
From the definition of
\mathscr{A}_{0}
, we compute

\mathscr{A}_{0}(\tilde{r}\partial_{\tilde{r}}f)=\tilde{r}\partial_{\tilde{r}}\mathscr{A}_{0}f+[\mathscr{A}_{0},\tilde{r}\partial_{\tilde{r}}]f,

(194)

with

\big[\mathscr{A}_{0},r\partial_{r}\big]=2\mathscr{A}_{0}-rU\partial_{r}-\frac{2(r^{2}-1)}{r^{2}+1}U.

We then write by integration by parts and the coercivity estimate (
188
),

\displaystyle\left|\tilde{\eta}\int\mathscr{A}_{0}\big(\tilde{r}\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\big)\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\right|=\left|\tilde{\eta}\int\Big[\tilde{r}\partial_{\tilde{r}}\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}+2\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}-rU\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}-\frac{2(\tilde{r}^{2}-1)}{\langle\tilde{r}\rangle^{2}}U\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\Big]\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu^{2}\left[\int|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}|^{2}\partial_{\tilde{r}}(\omega_{0})d\tilde{r}+\|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}}+\int\left(\frac{|\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}|^{2}}{\langle\tilde{r}\rangle^{2}}+\frac{|\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}|^{2}}{\langle\tilde{r}\rangle^{8}}\right)\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\right]\lesssim\nu^{2}\|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}}.

The small linear and nonlinear term:
By definition, we have

\mathscr{A}_{0}(fg)=g\mathscr{A}_{0}f+[\mathscr{A}_{0},g]f\quad\textup{with}\quad[\mathscr{A}_{0},g]=2\partial_{\tilde{r}}g\partial_{\tilde{r}}-\Big[1-Q\Big]\frac{\partial_{\tilde{r}}g}{r},

(195)

and

\mathscr{A}_{0}(\partial_{\tilde{r}}f)=\partial_{\tilde{r}}\mathscr{A}_{0}f+[\mathscr{A}_{0},\partial_{\tilde{r}}]f,

(196)

with

[\mathscr{A}_{0},\partial_{\tilde{r}}]=\left(\frac{3\tilde{r}^{4}-6\tilde{r}^{2}-1}{8\tilde{r}^{2}}\right)U\partial_{\tilde{r}}+\frac{4\tilde{r}}{(1+\tilde{r}^{2})}U.

This gives the formula

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{0}\Big(\frac{\partial_{\tilde{r}}\big(\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}F\big)}{\tilde{r}}\Big)

\displaystyle=\mathscr{A}_{0}\left(\frac{F}{\tilde{r}}\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}+\frac{\partial_{\tilde{r}}F}{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\right)=\mathscr{A}_{0}(\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*})\frac{F}{\tilde{r}}+\big[\mathscr{A}_{0},\frac{F}{\tilde{r}}\big]\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}+\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{\partial_{\tilde{r}}F}{\tilde{r}}+\big[\mathscr{A}_{0},\frac{\partial_{\tilde{r}}F}{\tilde{r}}\big]\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}

\displaystyle=\Big[\partial_{\tilde{r}}\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}+\big[\mathscr{A}_{0},\partial_{\tilde{r}}\big]\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\Big]\frac{F}{\tilde{r}}+\big[\mathscr{A}_{0},\frac{F}{\tilde{r}}\big]\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}+\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{\partial_{\tilde{r}}F}{\tilde{r}}+\big[\mathscr{A}_{0},\frac{\partial_{\tilde{r}}F}{\tilde{r}}\big]\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}.

We then write by integration by parts

\displaystyle\int\mathscr{A}_{0}\Big(\frac{\partial_{\tilde{r}}\big(\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}F\big)}{\tilde{r}}\Big)\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}

\displaystyle=\int|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}|^{2}\left[-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\tilde{r}}{\omega_{0}}\partial_{\tilde{r}}\left(\frac{F\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}^{2}}\right)+\frac{\partial_{\tilde{r}}F}{\tilde{r}}\right]\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}

\displaystyle\quad+\int\left[\big[\mathscr{A}_{0},\partial_{\tilde{r}}\big]\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{F}{\tilde{r}}+\big[\mathscr{A}_{0},\frac{F}{\tilde{r}}\big]\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}+\big[\mathscr{A}_{0},\frac{\partial_{\tilde{r}}F}{\tilde{r}}\big]\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\right]\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}.

By the definition of the commutators, we observe

\left|-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\tilde{r}}{\omega_{0}}\partial_{\tilde{r}}\left(\frac{F\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}^{2}}\right)+\frac{\partial_{\tilde{r}}F}{\tilde{r}}\right|\lesssim\frac{|\partial_{\tilde{r}}F|}{\tilde{r}}+\frac{|F|}{\tilde{r}^{2}},

and

\displaystyle\left|\big[\mathscr{A}_{0},\partial_{\tilde{r}}\big]\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{F}{\tilde{r}}+\big[\mathscr{A}_{0},\frac{F}{\tilde{r}}\big]\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}+\big[\mathscr{A}_{0},\frac{\partial_{\tilde{r}}F}{\tilde{r}}\big]\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\right|

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\sum_{i=0}^{1}\frac{|\partial_{\tilde{r}}^{i}F|}{\tilde{r}^{2-i}}|\partial_{\tilde{r}}^{2}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}|+\sum_{i=0}^{2}\frac{|\partial_{\tilde{r}}^{i}F|}{\tilde{r}^{3-i}}|\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}|+\left(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{|\partial_{\tilde{r}}^{i}F|}{\tilde{r}^{4-i}}+\frac{|F|}{\tilde{r}\langle\tilde{r}\rangle^{5}}\right)|\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}|.

Now, consider
F=2\tilde{P}_{1}-2\tilde{N}_{1}+\tilde{m}_{v}
, we estimate from the definition of
\tilde{P}_{1}
and
\tilde{N}_{1}
and the pointwise bound (
134
) for
k=0,1
and
\tilde{r}\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}
,

\displaystyle\frac{|\partial_{\tilde{r}}F|}{\tilde{r}}+\frac{|F|}{\tilde{r}^{2}}\lesssim\nu^{2},\quad\big|(\tilde{r}\partial_{\tilde{r}})^{k}F\big|\lesssim\nu^{2}|\ln\langle\tilde{r}\rangle|.

Hence, we have by Cauchy-Schwarz and (
188
),

\displaystyle\left|\int\mathscr{A}_{0}\Big(\frac{\partial_{\tilde{r}}\big(\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}F\big)}{\tilde{r}}\Big)\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\right|\lesssim\nu^{2}\|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}}.

The cut-off term:
From the expression (
186
) of
T(\tilde{m}_{v})
, we compute

\displaystyle\mathscr{A}_{0}T(\tilde{m}_{v})=-2\mathscr{A}_{0}\big(\partial_{\tilde{r}}\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}\big)+\mathscr{A}_{0}\big(F\tilde{m}_{v}\big)

\displaystyle=-2\partial_{\tilde{r}}\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\partial_{\tilde{r}}\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}+F\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}-2\partial_{\tilde{r}}\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\big[\mathscr{A}_{0},\partial_{\tilde{r}}\big]\tilde{m}_{v}

\displaystyle\qquad+\big[\mathscr{A}_{0},\partial_{\tilde{r}}\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\big]\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}+\big[\mathscr{A}_{0},F\big]\tilde{m}_{v},

where

F=-\Big(\partial_{\tilde{r}}^{2}+\partial_{s}\big)\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}+\partial_{\tilde{r}}\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\left[\frac{1}{\tilde{r}}-\frac{\big[2Q+2\tilde{P}_{1}-2\tilde{N}_{1}+\tilde{m}_{v}\big]}{2\tilde{r}}+\tilde{\eta}\;\tilde{r}\right].

By definition, we have

\left|(\tilde{r}\partial_{\tilde{r}}+\tilde{r}^{2}\partial_{\tilde{r}}^{2})\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\right|\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}\leq\tilde{r}\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}\}},\quad\left|\partial_{s}\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\right|\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}\mathbf{1}_{\{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}\leq\tilde{r}\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}\}},

(197)

which gives the estimate

|\partial_{\tilde{r}}^{k}F|\lesssim\left(\frac{1}{\tilde{r}^{2+k}}+\left|\frac{\tilde{\nu}_{s}}{\tilde{\nu}}\right|\frac{1}{\tilde{r}^{k}}\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}\leq\tilde{r}\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}\}}\lesssim\frac{1}{\tilde{r}^{2+k}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}\leq\tilde{r}\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}\}}.

(198)

Since the cut-off term is localized in the zone
\tilde{r}\sim\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}
, we can use the mid-range control (
125
) to obtain a better estimate. More precisely, we use the decomposition
88
, i.e.

\tilde{m}_{v}=Q(\tilde{r})-Q(r)+\tilde{P}_{1}(\tilde{r})-P_{1}(r)-\tilde{N}_{1}(\tilde{r})-P_{2}(r)+m_{q}.

A direct computation and
\nu\sim\tilde{\nu}
( see Lemma
4.6
) yield

\displaystyle k=0,1,2,\quad\big|(\tilde{r}\partial_{\tilde{r}})^{k}\big[Q(\tilde{r})-Q(r)\big]\big|\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|\langle\tilde{r}\rangle^{2}}.

By the definition of
P_{1}
and
\tilde{P}_{1}
and
P_{2}
, we have the rough bound for
\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}\leq\tilde{r}\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}
,

\displaystyle k=0,1,2,\quad\big|(\tilde{r}\partial_{\tilde{r}})^{k}\big[\tilde{P}_{1}(\tilde{r})-P_{1}(r)-\tilde{N}_{1}(\tilde{r})-P_{2}(r)\big]\big|\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}.

Combining with the mid-range estimate (
124
) yields

\int|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}|^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}\leq\tilde{r}\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}\}}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\lesssim\frac{\nu^{4}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}+\|m_{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}(\zeta_{*}\leq\zeta\leq\zeta^{*})}^{2}.

(199)

Using this estimate and integration by parts, we estimate

\displaystyle\left|\int\left(-2\partial_{\tilde{r}}\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\partial_{\tilde{r}}\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}+F\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}\right)\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\right|=\left|\int|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}|^{2}\left[\frac{\tilde{r}}{\omega_{0}}\partial_{\tilde{r}}\Big(\partial_{\tilde{r}}\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}\Big)+F\right]\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\int|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}|^{2}\left(\frac{1}{\tilde{r}^{2}}+\left|\frac{\tilde{\nu}_{s}}{\tilde{\nu}}\right|\right)\mathbf{1}_{\{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}\leq\tilde{r}\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}\}}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\lesssim\frac{\nu^{6}}{\zeta_{*}^{2}|\ln\nu|^{2}}+\frac{\nu^{2}}{\zeta_{*}^{2}}\|m_{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}(\zeta_{*}\leq\zeta\leq\zeta^{*})}^{2}.

We also have by the commutator formulas (
195
) and (
196
),

\displaystyle\left|-2\partial_{\tilde{r}}\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\big[\mathscr{A}_{0},\partial_{\tilde{r}}\big]\tilde{m}_{v}+\big[\mathscr{A}_{0},\partial_{\tilde{r}}\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\big]\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}+\big[\mathscr{A}_{0},F\big]\tilde{m}_{v}\right|\lesssim\sum_{i=0}^{2}\frac{|\partial_{\tilde{r}}^{i}\tilde{m}_{v}|}{\tilde{r}^{4-i}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}\leq\tilde{r}\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}\}}.

from which and Cauchy-Schwarz and (
199
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\int\left(-2\partial_{\tilde{r}}\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\big[\mathscr{A}_{0},\partial_{\tilde{r}}\big]\tilde{m}_{v}+\big[\mathscr{A}_{0},\partial_{\tilde{r}}\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\big]\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}+\big[\mathscr{A}_{0},F\big]\tilde{m}_{v}\right)\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\int|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}|^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}\leq\tilde{r}\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}\}}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int\frac{1}{\tilde{r}^{4}}\Big[|\partial_{\tilde{r}}^{2}\tilde{m}_{v}|^{2}+\frac{|\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}|^{2}}{\tilde{r}^{2}}+\frac{|\tilde{m}_{v}|^{2}}{\tilde{r}^{4}}\Big]\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{\zeta_{*}^{2}}\left(\frac{\nu^{4}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}+\|m_{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}(\zeta_{*}\leq\zeta\leq\zeta^{*})}\right).

Injecting the above estimates into (
192
) and using
\frac{ds}{d\tau}=\frac{1}{\tilde{\nu}^{2}}
and
\nu\sim\tilde{\nu}
yields (
190
).

Step 2

Energy estimate for the first order derivative
. We now prove (
191
). In order to handle the term
\|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}}^{2}
appearing in (
190
), we use the second energy identity by integrating equation (
183
) against
-\tilde{\nu}^{2}\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}
in
L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}
to write

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{ds}\left[-\tilde{\nu}^{2}\int\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\right]

\displaystyle=-\tilde{\nu}^{2}\int|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}|^{2}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}-\tilde{\nu}^{2}\int\mathscr{A}_{0}\big(\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\mathcal{E}_{1}\big)\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}

\displaystyle\quad-\tilde{\nu}^{2}\int\Big[\mathcal{F}(\tilde{m}_{v})+T(\tilde{m}_{v})+\frac{\tilde{\nu}_{s}}{\tilde{\nu}}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\Big]\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}.

Since the support of
\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}
is in the interval
0\leq\tilde{r}\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}
, we have by Hardy inequality (
275
) and (
188
),

\int\frac{|\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}|^{2}}{1+\tilde{r}^{2}}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\lesssim\int|\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}|^{2}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\lesssim\frac{\zeta_{*}^{2}}{\tilde{\nu}^{2}}\int\frac{|\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}|^{2}}{1+\tilde{r}^{2}}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\lesssim\frac{\zeta_{*}^{2}}{\tilde{\nu}^{2}}\int|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}|^{2}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r},

from which and Cauchy-Schwarz and (
193
), we estimate

\displaystyle\left|\tilde{\nu}^{2}\int\mathscr{A}_{0}\big(\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\mathcal{E}_{1}\big)\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\right|\lesssim\tilde{\nu}^{4}\int\frac{|\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}|^{2}}{1+\tilde{r}^{2}}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}+\frac{\zeta_{*}^{2}}{\tilde{\nu}^{2}}\int|\mathscr{A}_{0}\big(\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\mathcal{E}_{1}\big)|^{2}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\zeta_{*}^{2}\tilde{\nu}^{2}\int\frac{|\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}|^{2}}{1+\tilde{r}^{2}}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}+\frac{\zeta_{*}^{2}}{\tilde{\nu}^{2}}\frac{\nu^{8}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\lesssim\zeta_{*}^{2}\tilde{\nu}^{2}\|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}}+\frac{\zeta_{*}^{2}\nu^{6}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}.

As for the scaling term, we simply estimate by using Cauchy-Schwarz and (
188
),

\displaystyle\left|\tilde{\nu}^{2}\tilde{\eta}\int\tilde{r}\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\tilde{\nu}^{2}|\tilde{\eta}|\|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}}\left(\int|\tilde{r}\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}|^{2}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\tilde{\nu}^{2}\zeta_{*}^{2}\|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}}.

By the definition of
\tilde{P}_{1}
,
\tilde{N}_{1}
and (
134
), we have the rough bound for
\tilde{r}\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}
and
k=0,1
,

\big|(\tilde{r}\partial_{\tilde{r}})^{k}((2\tilde{P}_{1}-2\tilde{N}_{1}+\tilde{m}_{v})\big|\lesssim\nu^{2}|\ln\langle\tilde{r}\rangle|,

from which and (
188
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\tilde{\nu}^{2}\int\frac{\partial_{\tilde{r}}\big[(2\tilde{P}_{1}-2\tilde{N}_{1}+\tilde{m}_{v})\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\big]}{2\tilde{r}}\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\right|\lesssim\tilde{\nu}^{4}|\ln\nu|^{C}\|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}}.

To estimate for the cut-off term, we use (
197
) and (
198
) to bound

|T(\tilde{m}_{v})|=\big|-2\partial_{\tilde{r}}\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}+F\tilde{m}_{v}\big|\lesssim\left[\frac{1}{\tilde{r}}|\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}|+\left(\frac{1}{\tilde{r}^{2}}+\frac{\tilde{\nu}_{s}}{\tilde{\nu}}\right)|\tilde{m}_{v}|\right]\mathbf{1}_{\{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}\leq\tilde{r}\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}\}}.

We then use Cauchy-Schwarz and (
199
) and
|\tilde{\nu}_{s}/\tilde{\nu}|\lesssim\nu^{2}/|\ln\nu|
to estimate

\displaystyle\left|\tilde{\nu}^{2}\int T(\tilde{m}_{v})\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\right|\lesssim\tilde{\nu}^{2}\left(\int|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}|^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\{\frac{\zeta_{8}}{\tilde{\nu}}\leq\tilde{r}\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}\}}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int\frac{1}{\tilde{r}^{2}}\Big[|\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{m}_{v}|^{2}+\frac{|\tilde{m}_{v}|^{2}}{\tilde{r}^{2}}\Big]\mathbf{1}_{\{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}\leq\tilde{r}\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}\}}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{\zeta_{*}^{2}}\left(\frac{\nu^{4}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}+\|m_{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}(\zeta_{*}\leq\zeta\leq\zeta^{*})}\right).

Since
|\tilde{\nu}_{s}/\tilde{\nu}|\lesssim\nu^{2}/|\ln\nu|^{2}
, we use the bootstrap estimate (
125
) to bound the last term

\left|\tilde{\nu}\tilde{\nu}_{s}\int\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\right|\lesssim\frac{\nu^{6}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}.

Summing up these estimates and taking
\zeta_{*}
small enough and using
\frac{ds}{d\tau}=\frac{1}{\tilde{\nu}^{2}}
and
\tilde{\nu}\sim\nu
yield the desired formula (
191
). This concludes the proof of Lemma
4.9
assuming (
193
).

Step 3

Bound for the error
. We finally prove (
193
). We recall from Proposition
2.1
the identity

(\mathscr{A}_{0}-\beta\tilde{\nu}^{2}\tilde{r}\partial_{\tilde{r}})\phi_{n}(\tilde{r})=2\beta\tilde{\nu}^{2}\big(1-n+\tilde{\alpha}_{n}(\tilde{\nu})\big)\phi_{n}(\tilde{r}).

We then rewrite the error term by using the relation
\tilde{r}\partial_{\tilde{r}}Q=8T_{0}\sim 8\phi_{0}
and
ds/d\tau=1/\tilde{\nu}^{2}
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1}(\tilde{r},s)

\displaystyle=\Big[-a_{1,\tau}+2\beta\tilde{\alpha}_{1}(\tilde{\nu})a_{1}\Big]\phi_{1}+\Big[a_{1,\tau}-2\beta(1+\tilde{\alpha}_{0}(\tilde{\nu}))a_{1}+8\eta\Big]\phi_{0}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{8\tilde{\nu}_{s}}{\tilde{\nu}}\tilde{\phi}_{0}+\frac{a_{1}}{\tilde{\nu}^{2}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\nu}_{s}}{\tilde{\nu}}\tilde{r}\partial_{\tilde{r}}(\phi_{1}-\phi_{0})-\partial_{s}(\phi_{1}-\phi_{0})\right)+\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{1}(\tilde{r},s),

where we used the definition of
\tilde{N}_{1}
, i.e,
\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{N}_{1}=\frac{\partial_{\tilde{r}}\tilde{P}_{1}^{2}}{2\tilde{r}}+8\tilde{\nu}^{2}\tilde{\phi}_{0}(\tilde{r})
with
\tilde{\phi}_{0}=\phi_{0}-T_{0}
to write

\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{1}(\tilde{r},s)=\big(\partial_{s}-\eta\tilde{r}\partial_{\tilde{r}}\big)\tilde{N}_{1}+\frac{\partial_{\tilde{r}}(2\tilde{P}_{1}\tilde{N}_{1}-\tilde{N}_{1}^{2})}{2\tilde{r}}+\tilde{N}_{0}(v^{\perp}).

We recall from Lemma
4.1
the modulation equations

\displaystyle a_{1,\tau}-2\beta a_{1}\tilde{\alpha}_{1}(\nu)=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\right),\qquad\left(a_{1,\tau}-2\beta(1+\tilde{\alpha}_{0}(\nu))a_{1}+8\nu^{2}(\frac{\nu_{\tau}}{\nu}-\beta)\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\right),

from which and from the estimate (
172
), we obtain

\displaystyle|a_{1,\tau}-2\beta a_{1}\tilde{\alpha}_{1}(\tilde{\nu})|=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\right),\qquad\left|\left(a_{1,\tau}+2\beta(1+\tilde{\alpha}_{0}(\tilde{\nu}))a_{1}-8\tilde{\nu}^{2}(\frac{\tilde{\nu}_{\tau}}{\tilde{\nu}}-\beta)\right)\right|=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}\right).

From (
66
) and (
68
), the identities
\mathscr{A}_{0}T_{0}=0
and
\mathscr{A}_{0}T_{1}=-T_{0}
and
\tilde{\nu}\sim\nu
, one has the bound for
r\leq\zeta^{*}/\tilde{\nu}
,

|\mathscr{A}_{0}\phi_{0}|+|\mathscr{A}_{0}\phi_{1}|\lesssim\tilde{\nu}^{2}\frac{\tilde{r}^{2}}{\langle\tilde{r}\rangle^{4}},

and hence, we have the estimate

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}|\mathscr{A}_{0}\left(\Big[-a_{1,\tau}+2\beta\tilde{\alpha}_{1}(\tilde{\nu})a_{1}\Big]\phi_{1}+\Big[a_{1,\tau}-2\beta(1+\tilde{\alpha}_{0}(\tilde{\nu}))a_{1}+8\eta\Big]\phi_{0}\right)|^{2}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\lesssim\frac{\nu^{8}}{|\ln\nu|^{4}}\int_{0}^{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\frac{1}{1+\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\lesssim\frac{\nu^{8}}{|\ln\nu|^{3}}.

From (
66
) and
|\tilde{\nu}_{s}/\tilde{\nu}|\lesssim\nu^{2}/|\ln\nu|
, we have the estimate

\left|\frac{\tilde{\nu}_{s}}{\tilde{\nu}}\right|^{2}\int_{0}^{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{\phi}_{0}|^{2}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\lesssim\frac{\nu^{8}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}.

By writing

\partial_{s}(\phi_{1}-\phi_{0})=\frac{\tilde{\nu}_{s}}{\tilde{\nu}}(\tilde{\nu}\partial_{\tilde{\nu}}(\phi_{1}-\phi_{0})+\frac{\beta_{s}}{\beta}(\beta\partial_{\beta}(\phi_{1}-\phi_{0}))

and using (
64
) and (
68
), we have the bound

|\mathscr{A}_{0}(\partial_{s}(\phi_{1}-\phi_{0}))|\lesssim\frac{\nu^{4}}{|\ln\nu|}\frac{\tilde{r}^{2}}{\langle\tilde{r}\rangle^{4}}.

We also have from (
66
) and (
68
) and
|\tilde{\nu}_{s}/\tilde{\nu}|\lesssim 1/|\ln\nu|
the bound

\left|\frac{\tilde{\nu}_{s}}{\tilde{\nu}}\mathscr{A}_{0}(\tilde{r}\partial_{\tilde{r}}(\phi_{1}-\phi_{0}))\right|\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}\frac{\tilde{r}^{2}}{\langle\tilde{r}\rangle^{4}}.

Hence, there holds the estimate

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}|\mathscr{A}_{0}\frac{a_{1}}{\tilde{\nu}^{2}}\left(\frac{\tilde{\nu}_{s}}{\tilde{\nu}}\tilde{r}\partial_{\tilde{r}}(\phi_{1}-\phi_{0})-\partial_{s}(\phi_{1}-\phi_{0})\right)|^{2}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\lesssim\frac{\nu^{8}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\int_{0}^{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}\left(\frac{1}{(1+\tilde{r})^{3}}+\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}(1+\tilde{r})}\right)\lesssim\frac{\nu^{8}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}.

As for the term
\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{1}
, we use the definitions of
\tilde{N}_{1}
and
\tilde{P}_{1}
and
\tilde{\nu}\sim\nu
and
|\tilde{\nu}_{s}/\tilde{\nu}|\lesssim\nu^{2}/|\ln\nu|
to bound for
\tilde{r}\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}
,

|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{1}(\tilde{r},s)|\lesssim\tilde{\nu}^{4}\left(\left|\frac{\tilde{\nu}_{s}}{\tilde{\nu}}\right|+\tilde{\nu}^{2}\right)\frac{\ln\langle\tilde{r}\rangle}{\langle\tilde{r}\rangle^{2}}\lesssim\nu^{6}\frac{\ln\langle\tilde{r}\rangle}{\langle\tilde{r}\rangle^{2}},

which gives

\int_{0}^{\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\tilde{\nu}}}|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{\mathcal{E}}_{1}|^{2}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}d\tilde{r}\lesssim\nu^{12}|\ln\nu|^{3}\lesssim\frac{\nu^{8}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}.

The collection of the above estimates concludes the proof of (
193
).
∎

4.5. Nonradial energy estimates

We begin with estimating the parameter
x^{*}
. We claim the following.

Lemma 4.10

(Estimate on
x^{*}
)
.

We have for
\tau_{0}
large enough

\left|\frac{x^{*}_{\tau}}{\mu}\right|^{2}\leq\frac{C}{\nu^{2}}\int_{|y|\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}\frac{|\nabla q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}dy+Ce^{-2\kappa\tau},

(200)

where
C>0
is independent from the bootstrap constants
\kappa,N,K,K^{\prime},K^{\prime\prime}
. In particular, we have the estimate

\left|\frac{x^{*}_{\tau}}{\mu}\right|\lesssim\frac{e^{-\kappa\tau}}{\nu^{2}}.

(201)

Proof.

We multiply equation (
144
) by
\partial_{z_{i}}U_{\nu}\rho_{0}
with
i=1,2
and
\rho_{0}=e^{-\beta|z|^{2}/2}
, and integrate over
\mathbb{R}^{2}
and use the orthogonality condition (
89
) to write

\displaystyle\frac{x^{*}_{i,\tau}}{\mu}\int\partial_{z_{i}}\big(U_{\nu}+\Psi_{\nu}\big)\partial_{z_{i}}U_{\nu}\rho_{0}dz=\int\left[-\mathscr{L}^{z}\varepsilon^{\perp}+\nabla\cdot\Big(\mathcal{G}(\varepsilon)-\frac{x^{*}_{\tau}}{\mu}\varepsilon^{0}\Big)-N^{\perp}(\varepsilon^{\perp})\right]\partial_{z_{i}}U_{\nu}\rho_{0}dz.

Using the definition of
U_{\nu}
and
\Psi_{\nu}
, we have

\int|\partial_{z_{i}}U_{\nu}|^{2}e^{-\beta\frac{|z|^{2}}{4}}dz=\frac{c_{0}}{\nu^{4}}\int\frac{r^{3}}{(1+r^{2})^{6}}e^{-\beta\nu^{2}\frac{r^{2}}{4}}dr\sim\frac{c_{0}^{\prime}}{\nu^{4}},

and

\left|\int\partial_{z_{i}}\Psi_{\nu}\partial_{z_{i}}U_{\nu}e^{-\beta\frac{|z|^{2}}{4}}dz\right|=\mathcal{O}(\nu^{-2}).

We write the linear operator as
\mathscr{L}_{0}q^{\perp}=\nabla\cdot(U\nabla\mathscr{M}q^{\perp})
, then by integration by parts and Cauchy-Schwarz and the decay of
U
we estimate

\displaystyle\left|\int\mathscr{L}^{z}\varepsilon^{\perp}\partial_{z_{i}}U_{\nu}\rho_{0}dz\right|=\left|\frac{1}{\nu^{5}}\int\nabla\cdot\big(U\nabla\mathscr{M}q^{\perp}\big)\partial_{y_{i}}U\rho dy-\frac{\beta}{\nu}\int\nabla\cdot(yq^{\perp})\partial_{y_{i}}U\rho dy\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{\nu^{5}}\int U\nabla\mathscr{M}q^{\perp}\cdot\nabla(\partial_{y_{i}}U\rho)dy+\frac{\beta}{\nu}\int q^{\perp}y\cdot\nabla(\partial_{y_{i}}U\rho)dy\right|

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\frac{1}{\nu^{5}}\left(\int_{|y|\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}U|\nabla\mathscr{M}q^{\perp}|^{2}\rho dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{|y|\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}U|\nabla(\partial_{y_{i}}U\rho)|^{2}\rho^{-1}dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{\nu^{6}}\left(\nu^{2}\int_{|y|\geq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}\frac{|q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}\rho dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{|y|\geq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}\left|\mathscr{M}\Big[\nabla\cdot(U\nabla(\partial_{y_{i}}U\rho))\Big]\right|^{2}\rho^{-1}dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+e^{-\kappa\tau}
