\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{\nu^{2}}\left(\nu^{2}\int\frac{|q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}\rho dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int\big|y\cdot\nabla(\partial_{y_{i}}U\rho)\big|^{2}\rho^{-1}dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\frac{1}{\nu^{5}}\left(\int_{|y|\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}U|\nabla\mathscr{M}q^{\perp}|^{2}\rho\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{1}{\nu^{6}}\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{0}\,\nu^{10}+\frac{1}{\nu^{2}}\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{0}\lesssim\frac{1}{\nu^{5}}\left(\int_{|y|\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}\frac{|\nabla q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}\rho\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{e^{-\kappa\tau}}{\nu^{2}}.

We write by the definition
\varepsilon^{0}=\frac{\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon}}{\zeta}
and use (
136
), (
137
) and the decay of
U
,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{x_{i,\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\int\partial_{z_{i}}\varepsilon\partial_{z_{i}}U_{\nu}\rho_{0}dz\right|=\left|\frac{x_{i,\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\int\left(\frac{\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon}}{\zeta}+\varepsilon^{\perp}\right)\partial_{z_{i}}\big(\partial_{z_{i}}U_{\nu}\rho_{0}\big)dz\right|

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\left|\frac{x_{i,\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\right|\frac{1}{\nu^{4}}\left(\int|\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon}|^{2}\frac{1}{\zeta U_{\nu}}d\zeta\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\left|\frac{x_{i,\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\right|\frac{e^{-\kappa\tau}}{\nu^{3}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\lesssim\left|\frac{x_{i,\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\right|\left(\frac{1}{\nu^{4}}\|\tilde{m}_{v}\|_{\textup{in}}+\frac{1}{\nu^{2}|\ln\nu|}\int_{\zeta\geq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2}}\zeta^{C}\rho_{0}d\zeta+\frac{e^{-\kappa\tau}}{\nu^{3}}\right)\lesssim\left|\frac{x_{i,\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\right|\frac{1}{\nu^{2}|\ln\nu|}.

We have by Cauchy-Schwarz,

\displaystyle\left|\int\nabla\cdot\mathcal{G}(\varepsilon^{\perp})\partial_{z_{i}}U_{\nu}\rho_{0}dz\right|=\frac{1}{\nu^{5}}\left|\int\nabla\cdot\mathcal{G}(q^{\perp})\partial_{y_{i}}U\rho dy\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\int\Big|\nabla\cdot\mathcal{G}(q^{\perp})\Big|^{2}\rho dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int|\partial_{y_{i}}U|^{2}\rho dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\frac{1}{\nu^{5}}\left(\int\Big|\nabla\cdot\mathcal{G}(q^{\perp})\Big|^{2}\rho_{0}dz\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},

where we recall from the definition of
\mathcal{G}(\varepsilon^{\perp})
,

\nabla\cdot\mathcal{G}(q^{\perp})=\nabla q^{\perp}\cdot\nabla\Phi_{\Phi+q^{0}}-2q^{\perp}(\Psi+q^{0})+\nabla(\Psi+q^{0})\cdot\nabla\Phi_{q^{\perp}},

and
\Psi
is given by

\Psi=\frac{a_{1}}{\nu^{2}}\frac{\partial_{r}}{r}(\phi_{1}-\phi_{0})+\sum_{n=2}^{N}\frac{a_{n}}{\nu^{2}}\frac{\partial_{r}}{r}\phi_{n}.

By the definition of
\phi_{n}
, we have the estimate

|\Psi(r)|+|r\partial_{r}\Psi(r)|\lesssim\nu^{2}\langle r\rangle^{-2}+\frac{\langle r\rangle^{-4}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\{r\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}\}}+\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\langle\nu r\rangle^{C}\mathbf{1}_{\{r\geq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}\}},

(202)

from which and (
137
) and (
138
), we obtain the bound

\int\Big|\nabla\cdot\mathcal{G}(q^{\perp})\Big|^{2}\rho dy\lesssim\int_{|y|\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}\left(\frac{|\nabla q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}+\frac{|q^{\perp}|^{2}}{\sqrt{U}}\right)dy+e^{-2\kappa\tau}.

By using (
127
), (
137
) and (
138
), we estimate the contribution from
N^{\perp}(\varepsilon^{\perp})
,

\int\Big|N^{\perp}(\varepsilon^{\perp})\Big|^{2}\rho_{0}dz\lesssim e^{-2\kappa\tau}.

The collection of the above estimates yields (
200
) and concludes the proof of Lemma
4.10
.
∎

We are now in the position to derive the energy estimate for the nonradial part. Let us begin with
\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{0}
by using the coercivity given in Proposition
2.4
.

Lemma 4.11

(Control of
\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{0}
)
.

We have

\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{d\tau}\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{0}^{2}\leq-\delta^{\prime}\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{0}^{2}+Ce^{-2\kappa\tau},

(203)

where
\delta^{\prime},C>0
are independent from the bootstrap constants
K,K^{\prime},K^{\prime\prime}
.

Proof.

We multiply equation (
144
) by
\nu^{2}\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\mathscr{M}^{z}(\varepsilon^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho_{0}})
with
\rho_{0}(z)=e^{-\beta|z|^{2}/2}
and integrate over
\mathbb{R}^{2}
to write

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{d\tau}\|\varepsilon^{\perp}(\tau)\|_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle=\nu^{2}\int\mathscr{L}^{z}\varepsilon^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\mathscr{M}^{z}(\varepsilon^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho_{0}})dz+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{d}{d\tau}(\nu^{2}U_{\nu}^{-1})|\varepsilon^{\perp}|^{2}\rho_{0}dz

\displaystyle-\nu^{2}\int\big[\nabla\cdot\mathcal{G}(\varepsilon^{\perp})-\frac{x^{*}_{\tau}}{\mu}\cdot\nabla(W+\varepsilon^{0})-N^{\perp}(\varepsilon^{\perp})\big]\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\mathscr{M}^{z}(\varepsilon^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho_{0}})dz.

We have the coercivity estimate (
78
) for the linear part, which using (
269
) with
\alpha=1
gives:

\displaystyle\nu^{2}\int\tilde{\mathscr{L}}^{z}\varepsilon^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\mathscr{M}^{z}(\varepsilon^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho_{0}})dz

\displaystyle=\int\tilde{\mathscr{L}}q^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho}\mathscr{M}(q^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho})dy\leq-\delta_{0}\int\frac{|\nabla q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}\rho dy

(204)

and

\displaystyle-\int\frac{|\nabla q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}\rho dy\leq-C_{0}\nu^{2}\int\frac{|q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}\rho dy=-C_{0}\nu^{2}\int\frac{|\varepsilon^{\perp}|^{2}}{U_{\nu}}\rho_{0}dz=-C_{0}\|\varepsilon\|^{2}_{0}.

(205)

The time derivative term:
We write by the definition of
U_{\nu}
,

\frac{d}{d\tau}(\nu^{2}U_{\nu}^{-1})=\frac{\nu_{\tau}}{\nu}\nu\partial_{\nu}[(|z^{2}|+\nu^{2})^{2}]=\frac{\nu_{\tau}}{\nu}\frac{4\nu^{2}}{U_{\nu}}\frac{\nu^{2}}{|z|^{2}+\nu^{2}},

from which and
\left|\frac{\nu_{\tau}}{\nu}\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|}
and (
205
), we have the estimate

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{d}{d\tau}(\nu^{2}U_{\nu}^{-1})|\varepsilon^{\perp}|^{2}\rho_{0}dz\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}\int\frac{|\varepsilon^{\perp}|^{2}}{U_{\nu}}\rho_{0}dz\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|}\int\frac{|\nabla q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}\rho dy.

The small linear and nonlinear terms
:
We write by integration by parts and Cauchy-Schwarz,

\displaystyle\left|\nu^{2}\int\nabla\cdot\mathcal{G}(\varepsilon^{\perp})\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\mathscr{M}^{z}(\varepsilon^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho_{0}})dz\right|=\left|\nu^{2}\int\mathcal{G}(\varepsilon)\cdot\Big[\nabla\mathscr{M}^{z}(\varepsilon^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho_{0}})-\frac{\beta}{2}z\mathscr{M}^{z}(\varepsilon^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho_{0}})\Big]\sqrt{\rho_{0}}dz\right|

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}}\int\left(U_{\nu}|\nabla\mathscr{M}^{z}(\varepsilon^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho_{0}})|^{2}+U_{\nu}|\mathscr{M}^{z}(\varepsilon^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho_{0}})|^{2}\right)dz+\nu^{2}\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}\int\frac{(1+|z|^{2})|\mathcal{G}(\varepsilon)|^{2}}{U_{\nu}}\rho_{0}dz.

Making a change of variables and using Hardy inequality (
268
) yields

\displaystyle\nu^{2}\int U_{\nu}|\nabla\mathscr{M}^{z}(\varepsilon^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho_{0}})|^{2}dz=\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}(q^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho})|^{2}dy\lesssim\int\frac{|\nabla q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}\rho dy.

We also have by (
205
),

\displaystyle\nu^{2}\int U_{\nu}|\mathscr{M}^{z}(\varepsilon^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho_{0}})|^{2}dz\lesssim\nu^{2}\int\frac{|\varepsilon^{\perp}|^{2}}{U_{\nu}}\rho_{0}dz\lesssim\int\frac{|\nabla q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}\rho dy.

(206)

We recall

\Psi_{\nu}+\varepsilon^{0}=\frac{\partial_{\zeta}}{\zeta}(P_{\nu}+m_{\varepsilon}),\quad\nabla\Phi_{\Psi_{\nu}+\varepsilon^{0}}=-z\frac{(P_{\nu}+m_{\varepsilon})}{\zeta^{2}},

where we have from the definition of
P_{\nu}
and (
134
), (
135
) and (
136
) the bound

|\zeta\partial_{\zeta}(P_{\nu}+m_{\varepsilon})|\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2}\}}+\nu^{2}|\ln\nu|\zeta^{\frac{1}{4}}\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta\geq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2}\}}.

We then estimate by using (
269
),

\displaystyle\nu^{2}\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}\int\frac{(1+|z|^{2})|\varepsilon^{\perp}\nabla\Phi_{\Psi_{\nu}+\varepsilon^{0}}|^{2}}{U_{\nu}}\rho_{0}dz\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}}\int_{|z|\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2}}\frac{|\varepsilon^{\perp}|^{2}}{U_{\nu}}\rho_{0}dz+\nu^{6}|\ln\nu|^{\frac{5}{2}}\int_{|z|\geq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2}}\frac{|\varepsilon^{\perp}|^{2}}{U_{\nu}}|z|^{\frac{5}{2}}\rho_{0}dz

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}}\int\frac{|q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}\rho dy+\nu^{6}|\ln\nu|^{\frac{5}{2}}\nu^{\frac{5}{2}}\int\frac{|q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}(1+|y|^{\frac{5}{2}})\rho dy\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}}\int\frac{|\nabla q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}\rho dy.

We recall
P_{1,\nu}=\frac{a_{1}}{\nu^{2}}(\phi_{1}(r)-\phi_{0}(r))
and the bound

\left|\Psi_{1,\nu}\right|=\left|\frac{\zeta\partial_{\zeta}P_{1,\nu}}{\zeta^{2}}\right|\lesssim\frac{|y|^{2}}{1+|y|^{4}},\quad\forall y\in\mathbb{R}^{2},

so that we write

\displaystyle\nu^{2}\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}\int\frac{(1+|z|^{2})|\Psi_{1,\nu}\nabla\Phi_{\varepsilon^{\perp}}|^{2}}{U_{\nu}}\rho_{0}dz\lesssim\nu^{4}\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}\int(1+\nu^{2}|y|^{2})\big(|\nabla\Phi_{q^{\perp}\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}}|^{2}+|\nabla\Phi_{q^{\perp}(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}})}|^{2}\big)\rho dy.

We estimate by using (
272
) and (
269
),

\displaystyle\nu^{4}\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}\int(1+\nu^{2}|y|^{2})|\nabla\Phi_{q^{\perp}\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}}|^{2}\rho dy

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\nu^{4}\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}\left[\int_{|y|\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}|q^{\perp}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{4}dy+\nu^{2}\int_{|y|\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}|q^{\perp}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{6}dy\right]\int\frac{1}{1+|y|^{4}}\rho dy\lesssim\nu^{2}\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}\int\frac{|\nabla q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}\rho dy.

Using (
273
) and noting that
w^{\perp}=\varepsilon^{\perp}
, we estimate

\displaystyle\nu^{4}\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}\int(1+\nu^{2}|y|^{2})|\nabla\Phi_{q^{\perp}(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}})}(y)|^{2}\rho dy=\nu^{4}\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}\int(1+|z|^{2})|\nabla\Phi_{\varepsilon^{\perp}(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2}})}(z)|^{2}\rho_{0}dz

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\nu^{4}\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}\int_{|z|\leq 1}|\nabla\Phi_{\varepsilon^{\perp}(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2}})}(z)|^{2}dz+\nu^{4}\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}\int_{|z|\geq 1}\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{\textup{ex}}^{2}|z|^{\frac{3}{2}}\rho_{0}dz\lesssim e^{-2\kappa\tau}.

We have by the definition of
P_{2,\nu}
and (
134
) and (
135
),

\displaystyle|\Psi_{2,\nu}+\varepsilon^{0}|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{\zeta\partial_{\zeta}(P_{2,\nu}+m_{\varepsilon})}{\zeta}\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{\nu^{2}|\ln\nu|}\frac{|y|^{2}\ln\langle|y|\rangle}{1+|y|^{4}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2}\}}+\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|}|z|^{-2+\frac{1}{4}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|z|\geq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2}\}},

from which and a similar estimate as given above, we obtain

\displaystyle\nu^{2}\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}\int\frac{(1+|z|^{2})|(\Psi_{2,\nu}+\varepsilon^{0})\nabla\Phi_{\varepsilon^{\perp}}|^{2}}{U_{\nu}}\rho_{0}dz\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}}\int\frac{|\nabla q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}\rho dy+e^{-2\kappa\tau}.

We also have by (
137
) and (
138
) and (
201
) the rough estimate

\displaystyle\nu^{2}\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}\int\frac{\left|\varepsilon^{\perp}\nabla\Phi_{\varepsilon^{\perp}}\right|^{2}+\left|\frac{x^{*}_{\tau}}{\mu}\varepsilon^{\perp}\right|^{2}}{U_{\nu}}\rho_{0}dz\lesssim\frac{e^{-4\kappa\tau}}{\nu^{12}}\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}\int(1+|z|^{C})\rho_{0}dz+\left|\frac{x^{*}_{\tau}}{\mu}\right|^{2}\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{0}^{2}\lesssim e^{-2\kappa\tau},

and from (
136
) and (
125
),

\displaystyle\nu^{2}\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}\left|\frac{x^{*}_{\tau}}{\mu}\right|^{2}\int\frac{|\varepsilon^{0}|^{2}}{U_{\nu}}\rho_{0}dz=\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}\left|\frac{x^{*}_{\tau}}{\mu}\right|^{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon}|^{2}\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}\left|\frac{x^{*}_{\tau}}{\mu}\right|\left[\int_{\zeta\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2}}|\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon}|^{2}\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta+\int_{\zeta\geq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2}}|\partial_{\zeta}m_{\varepsilon}|^{2}\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}d\zeta\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\sqrt{|\ln\nu|}\left|\frac{x^{*}_{\tau}}{\mu}\right|\left[\|\tilde{m}_{v}\|^{2}_{\textup{in}}+\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}\int_{\zeta\geq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2}}\zeta^{C}e^{-\beta\zeta^{2}/2}d\zeta\right]\lesssim e^{-2\kappa\tau}.

The difference
(\mathscr{L}^{z}-\tilde{\mathscr{L}}^{z})\varepsilon^{\perp}
:
By the definition
\tilde{\Phi}_{q^{\perp}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\rho}}\Phi_{q^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho}}
, we have

\displaystyle\Delta(\Phi_{q^{\perp}}-\tilde{\Phi}_{q^{\perp}})=\beta\nu^{2}\left(1+\frac{\beta\nu^{2}|y|^{2}}{4}\right)\tilde{\Phi}_{q^{\perp}}-b\nu^{2}y\cdot\nabla\tilde{\Phi}_{q^{\perp}},

from which and an integration by parts and Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we write

\displaystyle\left|\nu^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\big(\mathscr{L}^{z}-\tilde{\mathscr{L}}^{z}\big)\varepsilon^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\mathscr{M}^{z}(\varepsilon^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho_{0}})dz\right|\ =\ \left|\nu^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\nabla U_{\nu}\cdot\nabla(\Phi_{\varepsilon^{\perp}}-\tilde{\Phi}_{\varepsilon^{\perp}})\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\mathscr{M}^{z}(\varepsilon^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho_{0}})dz\right|

\displaystyle\quad=\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\nabla U\cdot\nabla(\Phi_{q^{\perp}}-\tilde{\Phi}_{q^{\perp}})\sqrt{\rho}\mathscr{M}(q^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho})dy\right|\ \leq\ \left(\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}(q^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho})|^{2}dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int U|\nabla(\Phi_{q^{\perp}}-\tilde{\Phi}_{q^{\perp}})|^{2}\rho dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\nu^{2}\left(\int U|\mathscr{M}(q^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho})|^{2}dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int U\Big(|y\cdot\nabla(\Phi_{q^{\perp}}-\tilde{\Phi}_{q^{\perp}})|^{2}+|\langle\nu y\rangle^{2}\Phi_{q^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho}}|^{2}\Big)\rho dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\frac{1}{50}\int\frac{|\nabla q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}\rho dy+C\int U\langle\nu y\rangle^{2}|\nabla(\Phi_{q^{\perp}}-\tilde{\Phi}_{q^{\perp}})|^{2}\rho dy+C\nu^{2}\int U\langle\nu y\rangle^{4}|\Phi_{q^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho}}|^{2}dy.

We bound the last term by using (
272
) with
\alpha=1,3
and (
269
),

\nu^{2}\int U\langle\nu y\rangle^{4}|\Phi_{q^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho}}|^{2}dy\lesssim\nu^{2}\int|q^{\perp}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2}\rho dy+\nu^{6}\int|q^{\perp}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{6}\rho dy\lesssim\nu^{2}\int\frac{|\nabla q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}\rho dy.

For
|y|\geq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}
, we use (
272
), (
269
) and (
273
) to estimate

\displaystyle\int_{|y|\geq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}U\langle\nu y\rangle^{2}|\nabla(\Phi_{q^{\perp}}-\tilde{\Phi}_{q^{\perp}})|^{2}\rho dy

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{|y|\geq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}U\langle\nu y\rangle^{2}\left(|\nabla\Phi_{q^{\perp}\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}}|^{2}\rho+|\nabla\Phi_{q^{\perp}(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}})}|^{2}\rho+\nu^{4}|y|^{2}|\Phi_{q^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho}}|^{2}+|\nabla\Phi_{q^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho}}|^{2}\right)dy

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\nu^{2}\int\frac{|\nabla q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}\rho dy+\nu^{2}\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{\textup{ex}}^{2}.

For
|y|\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}
, we write by the definition
\tilde{\Phi}_{q^{\perp}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\rho}}\Phi_{q^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho}}
,

\displaystyle\int_{|y|\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}U(1+\nu^{2}|y|^{2})|\nabla(\Phi_{q^{\perp}}-\tilde{\Phi}_{q^{\perp}})|^{2}\rho dy\lesssim\int_{|y|\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}\frac{|\nabla\Phi_{q^{\perp}(1-\sqrt{\rho})}|^{2}+\nu^{4}(|y\Phi_{q^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho}}|^{2}+||y|^{2}\nabla\Phi_{q^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho}}|^{2}}{1+|y|^{4}}dy.

The last two terms are estimated by using (
272
) with
\alpha=1
and (
269
),

\displaystyle\int_{|y|\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}\frac{\nu^{4}(|y\Phi_{q^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho}}|^{2}+||y|^{2}\nabla\Phi_{q^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho}})|^{2}}{\langle y\rangle^{4}}dy\lesssim\nu^{2}\left(\int|q^{\perp}|^{2}\langle y\rangle^{2}\rho dy\right)\int_{|y|\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}\frac{1}{\langle y\rangle^{4}}dy\lesssim\nu^{2}\int\frac{|\nabla q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}\rho dy.

For the remaining term, we split into two parts

\displaystyle\int_{|y|\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}U(1+\nu^{2}|y|^{2})|\nabla\Phi_{q^{\perp}(1-\sqrt{\rho})}|^{2}dy\lesssim\int_{|y|\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}\frac{|\nabla\Phi_{q^{\perp}(1-\sqrt{\rho})\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}}|^{2}+|\nabla\Phi_{q^{\perp}(1-\sqrt{\rho})(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}})}|^{2}}{1+|y|^{4}}dy.

Since
(1-\sqrt{\rho})\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}(x)\lesssim\zeta_{*}^{2}\mathbf{1}_{|x|\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}
, we estimate by using (
272
) with
\alpha=1
and (
269
),

\displaystyle\int_{|y|\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}\frac{|\nabla\Phi_{q^{\perp}(1-\sqrt{\rho})\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}}(y)|^{2}}{1+|y|^{4}}dy\lesssim\zeta^{2}_{*}\int_{|y|\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}|q^{\perp}|^{2}(1+|y|^{2})dy\lesssim\zeta^{2}_{*}\int\frac{|\nabla q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}\rho dy.

Since
(1-\sqrt{\rho})(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}(x))\leq\mathbf{1}_{|x|\geq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}
, and
\frac{1}{|x-y|}\lesssim\frac{\nu}{\zeta_{*}}
for
|x|\geq\frac{\zeta^{*}}{\nu}
and
|y|\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}
, we estimate by using the outer norm (
130
),

\displaystyle\int_{|y|\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}\frac{|\nabla\Phi_{q^{\perp}(1-\sqrt{\rho})(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}})}(y)|^{2}}{1+|y|^{4}}dy\lesssim\int_{|y|\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}\frac{dy}{1+|y|^{4}}\left(\int_{|x|\geq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}\frac{|q^{\perp}|}{|x-y|}dx\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\nu^{2}\left(\int_{|z|\geq\zeta_{*}}\frac{|\varepsilon^{\perp}|}{|z|}dz\right)^{2}\lesssim\nu^{2}\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|^{2}_{\textup{ex}}\left(\int_{|z|\geq\zeta_{*}}\frac{dz}{|z|^{(3-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{1}{p})p^{\prime}}}\right)^{\frac{2}{p^{\prime}}}\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{\sqrt{\zeta_{*}}}\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|^{2}_{\textup{ex}}.

Gathering these obtained estimates and using the bootstrap bound (
130
) yields

\displaystyle\left|\nu^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\big(\mathscr{L}^{z}-\tilde{\mathscr{L}}^{z}\big)\varepsilon^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\mathscr{M}^{z}(\varepsilon^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho_{0}})dz\right|\lesssim\zeta_{*}^{2}\int\frac{|\nabla q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}\rho dy+e^{-2\kappa\tau}.

The error transport term
:
It remains to estimate the error term by writing

\displaystyle\frac{x_{\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\nu^{2}\cdot\int\nabla W\sqrt{\rho_{0}}\mathscr{M}^{z}(\varepsilon^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho_{0}})dz=\frac{x_{\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\nu\cdot\int\nabla V\sqrt{\rho}\mathscr{M}(q^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho})dy,

where
V(r,\tau)=\frac{1}{\nu^{2}}W(\zeta,\tau)
is the approximate profile in the blowup variables defined from (
84
), i.e,

V=U+\Psi,\quad\Psi=\frac{a_{1}}{\nu^{2}}\frac{\partial_{r}}{r}(\phi_{1}-\phi_{0})+\sum_{n=2}^{N}\frac{a_{n}}{\nu^{2}}\frac{\partial_{r}}{r}\phi_{n}.

By the definition of
\phi_{n}
, we have the estimate

|\Psi(r)|+|r\partial_{r}\Psi(r)|\lesssim\nu^{2}\langle r\rangle^{-2}+\frac{\langle r\rangle^{-4}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\{r\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\nu}\}}+\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\langle\nu r\rangle^{C}\mathbf{1}_{\{r\geq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\nu}\}}.

We use
\mathscr{M}\nabla U=0
and (
200
) to estimate the leading term

\displaystyle\left|\frac{x_{\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\nu\int\nabla U\sqrt{\rho}\mathscr{M}(q^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho})dy\right|=\left|\int\mathscr{M}\big(\nabla U(\sqrt{\rho}-1)\big)q^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho}dy\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\frac{x_{\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\right|\nu\left[\int_{|y|\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}\nu^{2}|y||q^{\perp}|\sqrt{\rho}dy+\int_{|y|\geq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}|\mathscr{M}\big(\nabla U(\sqrt{\rho}-1)\big)q^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho}|dy\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\frac{x_{\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\right|\nu^{2}\left(\int_{|y|\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}|q^{\perp}|^{2}(1+|y|^{2})\rho dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\frac{x_{\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\right|\nu\left(\int_{|y|\geq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}|q^{\perp}|^{2}(1+|y|^{2})\rho dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{|y|\geq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}\frac{|\mathscr{M}(\nabla U(\sqrt{\rho}-1))|^{2}}{1+|y|^{2}}dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\frac{x_{\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\right|\nu^{2}\left(\int|q^{\perp}|^{2}(1+|y|^{2})\rho dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\nu\int\frac{|\nabla q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}\rho dy+Ce^{-2\kappa\tau}.

Using (
202
) and (
200
) and (
137
), we estimate

\displaystyle\left|\frac{x_{\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\nu\int\nabla\Psi\sqrt{\rho}\mathscr{M}(q^{\perp}\sqrt{\rho})dy\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\frac{x_{\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\nu\right|\left(\int_{|y|\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2}}|\nabla\Psi|^{2}(1+|y|^{6})\rho\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{|y|\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}}|q^{\perp}|^{2}(1+|y|^{2})\rho\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{e^{-2\kappa\tau}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\left(\int\frac{|\nabla q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}\rho dy+\int|q^{\perp}|^{2}(1+|y|^{2})\rho dy\right)+\frac{e^{-2\kappa\tau}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\int\frac{|\nabla q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}\rho dy+Ce^{-2\kappa\tau}.

The collection of all the above estimates, using the control (
205
) and the coercivity (
204
), yields (
203
). This concludes the proof of Lemma
4.11
.
∎

We are now going to establish the monotonicity formula to control the inner norm (
129
). The basic idea is that inside the blowup zone
|y|\leq\frac{\zeta_{*}}{2\nu}
for
\zeta_{*}\ll 1
, the dynamic of (
208
) resembles
\partial_{s}q^{\perp}=\mathscr{L}_{0}q^{\perp}
which allows us to use the special structure of
\mathscr{L}_{0}
, namely that
\mathscr{L}_{0}f=\nabla\cdot(U\nabla\mathscr{M}f)
. We recall from (
144
), the equation satisfied by
q^{\perp}
,

\partial_{s}q^{\perp}=\mathscr{L}_{0}q^{\perp}+\eta\Lambda q^{\perp}-\nabla\cdot\mathcal{G}(q^{\perp})+\frac{\nu x^{*}_{\tau}}{\mu}\cdot\nabla(V+q^{0})+N^{\perp}(q^{\perp}),

(207)

where
\mathcal{G}(q^{\perp})
and
N^{\perp}(q^{\perp})
are defined as in (
145
), and
V
is the approximate solution in the blowup variables given by (
84
), namely that

V=U+\Psi,

where we recall

\Psi=\Psi_{1}+\Psi_{2},\quad\Psi_{1}=\frac{a_{1}}{\nu^{2}}(\varphi_{1}(r)-\varphi_{0}(r)),\quad\Psi_{2}=\sum_{n=2}^{N}\frac{a_{n}}{\nu^{2}}\varphi_{n}(r),

and
\varphi_{n}
is the radial eigenfunction of
\mathscr{L}_{0}
, namely that

\varphi_{n}(r)=-\frac{\partial_{r}\phi_{n}}{r},\quad\partial_{r}\varphi_{n}=-\frac{\phi_{n}}{r},

with
\phi_{n}
’s are the eigenfunctions of
\mathscr{A}
, and we write for short

\eta=\frac{\nu_{s}}{\nu}-\beta\nu^{2}=\mathcal{O}(\nu^{2}),\quad\Lambda f=\nabla\cdot(yf).

We define

q^{\perp}_{*}=\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}q^{\perp},

where
\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}
is defined as in (
15
) and
q^{\perp}_{*}
solves the equation

\displaystyle\partial_{s}q^{\perp}_{*}

\displaystyle=\mathscr{L}_{0}q^{\perp}_{*}+\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}\Big[\eta\nabla\cdot(yq^{\perp})-\nabla\cdot\mathcal{G}(q)+\frac{\nu x^{*}_{\tau}}{\mu}\cdot\nabla(V+q^{0})+N^{\perp}(q^{\perp})\Big]-[\mathscr{L}_{0},\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}]q^{\perp}+\partial_{s}\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}q^{\perp},

(208)

Consider the decomposition

q_{*}^{\bot}=c_{1}\partial_{1}U_{1}+c_{2}\partial_{2}U_{2}+\tilde{q}_{*}^{\bot}\quad\textup{with}\quad c_{i}=\frac{\int q_{*}^{\bot}\partial_{i}Udy}{\int|\partial_{i}U|^{2}dy}=\mathcal{O}(\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{0}),

(209)

which produces the orthogonality condition

\int\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}\partial_{1}Udy=\int\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}\partial_{0}Udy=0.

From Lemma
2.3
, we have the coercivity

-\int\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy=\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}dy\geq\delta_{2}\int\frac{|\nabla\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|}{U}dy,

(210)

and the positivity since
\int\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy=0
,

\int\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}\mathscr{M}\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy\geq 0.

(211)

Recall that
\mathscr{M}\partial_{1}U=\mathscr{M}\partial_{2}U=0
, hence, we have

\mathscr{M}q_{*}^{\perp}=\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp},\quad\mathscr{L}_{0}q_{*}^{\perp}=\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}.

(212)

Thanks to this coercivity, we are able to establish the monontonicity formula to control
\|q^{\perp}\|_{\textup{in}}
.

Lemma 4.12

(Control of
\|q^{\perp}\|_{\textup{in}}
)
.

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\tau}\left[\int\tilde{q}^{\perp}_{*}\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy-\int\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}^{\perp}_{*}\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy\right]

\displaystyle\leq-\delta_{2}^{\prime}\left[\frac{1}{\zeta_{*}^{2}}\int\tilde{q}^{\perp}_{*}\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy-\int\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}^{\perp}_{*}\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{C}{\nu^{2}}\Big(\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|^{2}_{0}+\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|^{2}_{H^{1}(\zeta_{*}\leq|z|\leq\zeta^{*})}+e^{-2\kappa\tau}\Big).

Proof.

We multiply equation (
208
) by
\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}
and
-\mathscr{M}\mathscr{L}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}
and integrate over
\mathbb{R}^{2}
and use the relation (
212
) and (
210
) and (
211
) to write the energy identity

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{ds}\left[\int\tilde{q}^{\perp}_{*}\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy-\int\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy\right]

\displaystyle\leq-\delta_{2}\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}dy+\int\left(\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}\Big[\eta\nabla\cdot(yq^{\perp})-\nabla\cdot\mathcal{G}(q^{\perp})+\frac{\nu x^{*}_{\tau}}{\mu}\cdot\nabla(V+q^{0})+N^{\perp}(q^{\perp})\Big]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\mathscr{M}\Big(\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}+\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}\Big)dy-\int\left([\mathscr{L}_{0},\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}]q^{\perp}-\partial_{s}\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}q^{\perp}\right)\mathscr{M}\Big(\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}+\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}\Big)dy.

Since we only use the positivity of
\mathscr{M}
, all the terms involving
\mathscr{M}\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}
are treated by using commutator formulas with
\mathscr{M}
in order to reduce the order of derivatives so that they can be controlled by
-\delta_{2}\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}dy
. In particular, we shall use the following formula: for any well localized function
f
without radial component,

[\mathscr{M},\Lambda]f=\frac{y\cdot\nabla U}{U^{2}}f+2\Phi_{f}\quad\textup{with}\quad\Lambda f=\nabla\cdot(yf),

(213)

where we used the identity
y\cdot\nabla\Phi_{f}=\Phi_{y\cdot\nabla f}+2\Phi_{f}
, and

[\mathscr{M},\nabla]f=\frac{\nabla U}{U^{2}}f,\quad[\mathscr{M},g]f=g\Phi_{f}-\Phi_{gf}.

(214)

The scaling term:
We write
\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}\nabla\cdot(yq^{\perp})=\nabla\cdot(yq^{\perp}_{*})-y\cdot\nabla\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}q^{\perp}
, and use (
213
) and the relation
\mathscr{M}q_{*}^{\perp}=\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}
and the structure
\mathscr{L}_{0}f=\nabla\cdot(U\nabla\mathscr{M}f)
and integration by parts, to estimate

\displaystyle\left|\eta\int\nabla\cdot(yq_{*}^{\perp})\mathscr{M}\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy\right|\lesssim\nu^{2}\left|\int\nabla\cdot(y\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp})\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy\right|+\nu^{2}\left|\int\Big(\frac{y\cdot\nabla U}{U^{2}}q_{*}^{\perp}+2\Phi_{q_{*}^{\perp}}\Big)\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu^{2}\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}\left(1+\frac{|\nabla\cdot(yU)|}{U}+\frac{|\nabla U|}{U}\right)dy+\nu^{2}\int U\left|\nabla\Big(\frac{y\cdot\nabla U}{U^{2}}q_{*}^{\perp}+2\Phi_{q_{*}^{\perp}}\Big)\right|^{2}dy

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu^{2}\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}dy+\nu^{2}\int\left(\frac{|\nabla q_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}+\frac{|\nabla U|^{2}}{U}|q_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}+U|\nabla\Phi_{q_{*}^{\perp}}|^{2}\right)dy

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu^{2}\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}dy+\nu^{2}\int\frac{|\nabla\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}dy+\nu^{2}(|c_{1}|^{2}+|c_{2}|^{2})\lesssim\nu^{2}\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}dy+\nu^{2}\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{0}^{2}.

where we used in the last line the decomposition (
209
) and the coercivity (
210
).

Since
y\cdot\nabla\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}\leq|y|\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\nu}\}}
, we use the midrange bootstrap (
128
) and integration by parts to estimate

\displaystyle\left|\eta\int y\cdot\nabla\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}q^{\perp}\mathscr{M}\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu^{2}\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}dy+\nu^{2}\int|\nabla\mathscr{M}\big(y\cdot\nabla\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}q^{\perp}\big)|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\nu^{2}\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}dy+\nu^{2}\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{H^{1}(\zeta_{*}\leq|z|\leq\zeta^{*})}^{2}.

We also estimate by integration by parts and Cauchy-Schwarz and
\big|y\cdot\nabla\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}\big|\leq\mathbf{1}_{\{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}\leq|y|\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\nu}\}}
,

\displaystyle\left|\eta\int\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}\nabla\cdot(yq^{\perp})\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy\right|=\left|\eta\int\Big(\nabla\cdot(yq_{*}^{\perp})-y\cdot\nabla\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}q^{\perp}\Big)\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy\right|

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{\delta_{2}}{100}\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}dy+\nu^{4}\int\frac{|yq_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}dy+\nu^{2}\int_{\frac{\zeta_{8}}{\nu}\leq|y|\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}\big|q^{\perp}\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}\big|dy

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\frac{\delta_{2}}{100}\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}dy+\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{0}^{2}+\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{H^{1}(\zeta_{*}\leq|z|\leq\zeta^{*})}^{2}.

Summing up these estimates yields

\displaystyle\left|\eta\int\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}\nabla\cdot(yq^{\perp})\mathscr{M}\big(\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}+\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}\big)dy\right|\leq\frac{\delta_{2}}{50}\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}dy+\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{H^{1}(\zeta_{*}\leq|z|\leq\zeta^{*})}^{2}+\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{0}^{2}.

The
\mathcal{G}(q^{\perp})
and
N^{\perp}
terms:
We write by integration by parts and Cauchy-Schwarz

\displaystyle\left|\int\Big(\nabla\cdot\mathcal{G}(q^{\perp})-N^{\perp}(q^{\perp})\Big)\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy\right|\leq\frac{\delta_{2}}{100}\int U|\mathscr{M}\nabla\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}dy+C\int_{|y|\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}\frac{|\mathcal{G}(q^{\perp})|^{2}+|N^{\perp}(q^{\perp})|^{2}}{U}dy

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\delta_{2}}{100}+\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\right)\int U|\mathscr{M}\nabla\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}dy+Ce^{-2\kappa\tau},

where we use (
202
) and the definition (
145
) of
\mathcal{G}(q^{\perp})
and
N^{\perp}(q^{\perp})
and (
137
), (
138
) to estimate

\displaystyle\int_{|y|\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}\frac{|\mathcal{G}(q^{\perp})|^{2}+|N^{\perp}(q^{\perp})|^{2}}{U}dy\lesssim\int_{|y|\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}\left(\frac{|q^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}\big|\Phi_{\Psi+q^{0}}\big|^{2}+|\nabla\Phi_{q^{\perp}}|^{2}\frac{|\Psi+q^{0}|^{2}}{U}+|q^{\perp}\nabla q^{\perp}|^{2}\right)dy

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\int_{|y|\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}|q^{\perp}|^{2}(1+|y|^{2})dy+e^{-2\kappa\tau}\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\int\frac{|\nabla\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}dy+\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{H^{1}(\zeta_{*}\leq|z|\leq\zeta^{*})}+e^{-2\kappa\tau}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\int U|\mathscr{M}\nabla\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}dy+Ce^{-2\kappa\tau}.

Similarly, we write by integration by parts and the definition of
\mathcal{G}
and
N^{\perp}
and Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\left|\int\mathscr{M}\Big(\nabla\cdot\mathcal{G}(q^{\perp})-N^{\perp}(q^{\perp})\Big)\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy\right|=\left|\int U\nabla\Big(\mathscr{M}\nabla\cdot\mathcal{G}(q^{\perp})-\mathscr{M}N^{\perp}(q^{\perp})\Big)\cdot\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\delta_{2}}{100}\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}dy+\left|\int U\nabla\mathscr{M}\Big(\nabla q^{\perp}\cdot\nabla\Phi_{\Psi+q^{0}}-2q^{\perp}(\Psi+q^{0})\Big)\cdot\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy\right|

\displaystyle\quad+C\int_{|y|\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\nabla(\Psi+q^{0})\cdot\nabla\Phi_{q^{\perp}})|^{2}+C\int_{|y|\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\nabla\cdot(q^{\perp}\nabla\Phi_{q^{\perp}})|^{2}dy.

Using the commutator formula (
214
) and (
202
) and the relation (
212
), we obtain the estimate

\displaystyle\left|\int U\nabla\mathscr{M}\Big(\nabla q^{\perp}\cdot\nabla\Phi_{\Psi+q^{0}}-2q^{\perp}(\Psi+q^{0})\Big)\cdot\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy\right|

\displaystyle\quad\leq\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}\left(|\Psi+q_{0}|+\frac{1}{2}\left|\frac{\nabla\cdot(U\nabla\Phi_{\Psi+q^{0}})}{U}\right|\right)dy

\displaystyle\qquad+\left|\int U\nabla\left([\mathscr{M},\Psi+q^{0}]q^{\perp}+[\mathscr{M},\nabla\Phi_{\Psi+q^{0}}]\cdot\nabla q^{\perp}+[\mathscr{M},\nabla]q^{\perp}\cdot\nabla\Phi_{\Psi+q^{0}}\right)\cdot\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}+\frac{\delta_{2}}{100}\right)\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+C\int_{|y|\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}U\Big|\nabla\left([\mathscr{M},\Psi+q^{0}]q^{\perp}+[\mathscr{M},\nabla\Phi_{\Psi+q^{0}}]\cdot\nabla q^{\perp}+[\mathscr{M},\nabla]q^{\perp}\cdot\nabla\Phi_{\Psi+q^{0}}\right)\Big|^{2}dy

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\leq\frac{\delta_{2}}{50}\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}+\frac{C}{|\ln\nu^{2}|}\left(\int|\nabla\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}(1+|y|^{4})+\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|^{2}_{H^{1}(\zeta_{*}\leq|z|\leq\zeta^{*})}\right).

From (
137
), (
138
) and (
202
) and (
272
) with
\alpha=1/2
, we estimate

\displaystyle\int_{|y|\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\nabla(\Psi+q^{0})\cdot\nabla\Phi_{q^{\perp}})|^{2}+\int_{|y|\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\nabla\cdot(q^{\perp}\nabla\Phi_{q^{\perp}})|^{2}dy

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\int|\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}(1+|y|^{2})dy+\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|^{2}_{H^{1}(\zeta_{*}\leq|z|\leq\zeta^{*})}+e^{-2\kappa\tau}.

Summing up these estimates yields

\displaystyle\left|\int\Big[\nabla\cdot\mathcal{G}(q^{\perp})-N^{\perp}(q^{\perp})\Big]\mathscr{M}\big(\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}+\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}\big)dy\right|\leq\frac{\delta_{2}}{50}\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}dy+\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{H^{1}(\zeta_{*}\leq|z|\leq\zeta^{*})}^{2}+e^{-2\kappa\tau}.

The error terms:
From (
202
) and (
134
), we note

|\Psi+q^{0}|+|r\partial_{r}(\Psi+q^{0})|\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\frac{1}{1+r^{4}},\quad\textup{for}\quad r\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\nu},

from which and the fact that
\mathscr{M}\nabla U=0
and (
200
), we obtain the estimate

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\nu x_{\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\cdot\int\nabla(V+q^{0})\mathscr{M}\big(\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}+\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}\big)dy\right|=\left|\frac{\nu x_{\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\cdot\int\mathscr{M}\nabla(\Psi+q^{0})\big(\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}+\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}\big)dy\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\int\frac{|\nabla\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}dy+\frac{C}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|^{2}_{H^{1}(\zeta_{*}\leq|z|\leq\zeta^{*})}+Ce^{-2\kappa\tau}.

We also have the estimate from (
201
) and Cauchy-Schwarz,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\nu x_{\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\cdot\int\nabla q^{\perp}\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy\right|\leq\left|\frac{\nu x_{\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\right|\left(\int_{|y|\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}|q^{\perp}|^{2}Udy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{\delta_{2}}{50}\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}dy+C\left|\frac{\nu x_{\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\right|^{2}\frac{\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{0}^{2}}{\nu^{2}}\leq\frac{\delta_{2}}{50}\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}dy+e^{-2\kappa\tau}.

Similarly, we use the commutator formula (
214
) to obtain the estimate

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\nu x_{\tau}^{*}}{\mu}\cdot\int\nabla q^{\perp}\mathscr{M}\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy\right|\leq\frac{\delta_{2}}{50}\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}dy+e^{-2\kappa\tau}.

The commutator terms:
Since the support of
[\mathscr{L}_{0},\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}]q^{\perp}
and
\partial_{s}\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}
is on
\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}\leq|y|\leq\frac{2\zeta_{*}}{\nu}
, we use the midrange bootstrap (
128
) and Cauchy-Schwarz to obtain the estimate

\displaystyle\left|\int\left([\mathscr{L}_{0},\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}]q^{\perp}-\partial_{s}\chi_{\frac{\zeta_{*}}{\nu}}q^{\perp}\right)\mathscr{M}\big(\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}+\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}\big)dy\right|\leq\frac{\delta_{2}}{50}\int U|\nabla\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}dy+\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{H^{1}(\zeta_{*}\leq|z|\leq\zeta^{*})}^{2}.

A collection of all the estimates and using the fact that

-\int\frac{|\nabla\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}}{U}dy\lesssim-\int|\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}|^{2}(1+|y|^{2})dy\lesssim-\frac{\nu^{2}}{\zeta_{*}^{2}}\int\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy,

and
\frac{ds}{d\tau}=\frac{1}{\nu^{2}}
yield the conclusion of Lemma
4.12
.
∎

4.6. Analysis in the exterior zone

In the exterior zone, we derive an energy estimate for the
\|\hat{w}\|_{\textup{ex}}
norm of the full higher order perturbation (
108
). From (
108
) and (
52
), the radial and non radial parts
\hat{w}^{0}
and
\hat{w}^{\perp}
of
\hat{w}
solve the following equations:

\partial_{\tau}\hat{w}^{0}+\beta\nabla.(z\hat{w}^{0})-\Delta\hat{w}^{0}=e.\nabla\hat{w}^{0}+f\hat{w}^{0}+g.\nabla\Phi_{(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{2}})\hat{w}^{0}}+h^{0}+N^{0}(\hat{w}^{\perp}),

(215)

\partial_{\tau}\hat{w}^{\perp}+\beta\nabla.(z\hat{w}^{\perp})-\Delta\hat{w}^{\perp}=e.\nabla\hat{w}^{\perp}+f\hat{w}^{\perp}+g.\nabla\Phi_{(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{2}})\hat{w}^{\perp}}+h^{\perp}+N^{\perp}(\hat{w}^{\perp}),

(216)

where

e=-\nabla\left(\Phi_{U_{\nu}}+\Phi_{\Psi_{\nu,1}}\right),\quad f=2(U_{\nu}+\Psi_{\nu,1}),\quad g=-\nabla(U_{\nu}+\Psi_{\nu,1}),

and from (
6
), (
66
), (
67
) and (
57
),

\displaystyle h^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\Big(-\partial_{\tau}f+\Delta f-\nabla\cdot(f\nabla\Phi_{f})-\beta\nabla\cdot(zf)\Big)

\displaystyle=\frac{\nu_{\tau}}{\nu}\nabla.(zU_{\nu})-\frac{\nu_{\tau}}{\nu}\nu\partial_{\nu}\Psi_{\nu,1}-\frac{\beta_{\tau}}{\beta}\beta\partial_{\beta}\Psi_{\nu,1}+(\alpha_{1}a_{1}-a_{1,\tau})(\varphi_{1}-\varphi_{0})

\displaystyle\quad+2\beta\left((\tilde{\alpha}_{1}-\tilde{\alpha}_{0})a_{1}-a_{1}-4\nu^{2}\right)\varphi_{0}+\beta\left(8\nu^{2}\varphi_{0}-\nabla.(zU_{\nu})\right)-\nabla(\Psi_{\nu,1}).\nabla\Phi_{\Psi_{\nu,1}}+\Psi_{\nu,1}^{2}+g.\nabla\Phi_{\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{2}}\hat{w}^{0}},

and

\displaystyle h^{\perp}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{x^{*}_{\tau}}{\mu}.\nabla\left(U_{\nu}+\Psi_{\nu,1}\right)+g.\nabla\Phi_{\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{2}}\hat{w}^{\perp}}.

From the pointwise estimates (
6
), (
66
), (
67
) on
U
,
\varphi_{0}
and
\varphi_{1}
, the Poisson formula for radial functions, we have the following estimates for
|z|\geq 1
and
k=0,1,2
:

|\partial_{\zeta}^{k}e(\zeta)|\lesssim\zeta^{-1+\delta-k},\quad|\partial_{\zeta}^{k}f(\zeta)|\lesssim\nu^{2}|\log\nu|\zeta^{-2+\delta-k},\quad|\partial_{\zeta}^{k}g(\zeta)|\lesssim\nu^{2}\zeta^{-3+\delta-k}.

(217)

We recall that
|\nu_{\tau}/\nu|\lesssim|\log\nu|^{-1}
and
|\beta_{\tau}/\beta|\lesssim|\log\nu|^{-3}
from (
147
), (
148
) and (
119
). Hence from (
67
), (
57
) and (
201
) we infer that for the forcing term for
|z|\geq 1
and
k=0,1,2
:

|\partial_{\zeta}^{k}h^{0}(\zeta)|\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\log\nu|}\zeta^{-2+\delta-k},\quad|\nabla^{k}h^{\perp}(\zeta)|\lesssim e^{-\kappa\tau}\nu\zeta^{-2+\delta-k}.

(218)

Lemma 4.13
.

There exists a universal constant
C>0
independent of the constants
N,\kappa,K,K^{\prime},K^{\prime\prime},\zeta^{*}
such that for
\tau_{0}
large enough:

\frac{d}{d\tau}\|\hat{w}^{0}\|_{\textup{ex}}^{2p}\leq 2p\left(-\frac{\beta}{4}+\frac{C}{\zeta^{*}}\right)\|\hat{w}^{0}\|_{\textup{ex}}^{2p}+C\frac{K^{{}^{\prime}2p}\nu^{4p}}{|\log\nu|^{2p}}+C\|\hat{w}^{0}\|_{\textup{ex}}^{2p-1}\frac{K\nu^{2}}{|\log\nu|}.

(219)

\frac{d}{d\tau}\|\hat{w}^{\perp}\|_{\textup{ex}}^{2p}\leq 2p\left(-\frac{\beta}{4}+\frac{C}{\zeta^{*}}\right)\|\hat{w}^{\perp}\|_{\textup{ex}}^{2p}+CK^{{}^{\prime}2p}e^{-2p\kappa\tau}+C\|\hat{w}^{\perp}\|_{\textup{ex}}^{2p-1}e^{-\kappa\tau}.

(220)

Proof.

Step 1

(Linear energy estimate)
. We claim that for any function
\bar{w}
, for any vector field
\bar{e}
and potential
\bar{f}
satisfying
|\bar{f}|\lesssim\zeta^{*-1}
and
|\bar{e}|+\zeta|\nabla\bar{e}|\lesssim 1
a constant
C>0
exists such that

\displaystyle\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\bar{w}^{2p-1}\left(-\beta\nabla.(z\bar{w})+\Delta\bar{w}+\bar{e}.\nabla\bar{w}+\bar{f}\bar{w}\right)\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}

(221)

\displaystyle\quad\leq-(2p-1)\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\bar{w}^{2p-2}|\nabla\bar{w}|^{2}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(-\frac{\beta}{4}+\frac{C}{\zeta^{*}}\right)\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\bar{w}^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}+C\|\bar{w}\|_{L^{2p}(\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}\leq|z|\leq\zeta^{*})}^{2p}.

We now prove this estimate. Integrating by parts, as derivatives of
\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}
have support in
\{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}\leq|z|\leq\zeta^{*}\}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)

\displaystyle\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\bar{w}^{2p-1}(-\beta\nabla.(z\bar{w}))\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}=-\frac{\beta}{4}\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\bar{w}^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}-\beta\int\partial_{\zeta}\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\bar{w}^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}},

and

\displaystyle\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\bar{w}^{2p-1}\Delta\bar{w}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}

\displaystyle=-(2p-1)\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\bar{w}^{2p-2}|\nabla\bar{w}|^{2}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}+\frac{1}{2p}\int\bar{w}^{2p}\Delta\left(\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p-2}\right)dz

\displaystyle\quad\leq-(2p-1)\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\bar{w}^{2p-2}|\nabla\bar{w}|^{2}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{2p}\int\bar{w}^{2p}\left(\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\Delta\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p-2}-2\nabla\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}.\nabla\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p-2}-\Delta\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p-2}\right)dz

\displaystyle\quad\leq-(2p-1)\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\bar{w}^{2p-2}|\nabla\bar{w}|^{2}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}+\frac{C}{|\zeta^{*}|^{2}}\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\bar{w}^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}+\mathcal{O}\left(\|\bar{w}\|_{L^{2p}(\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}\leq|z|\leq\zeta^{*})}^{2p}\right).

Using the bounds
|\bar{f}|\lesssim\zeta^{*-1}
and
|\bar{e}|+\zeta|\nabla\bar{e}|\lesssim 1
yield

\displaystyle\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\bar{w}^{2p-1}e.\nabla\bar{w}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}=-\frac{1}{2p}\int\bar{w}^{2p}\left(\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\nabla.\left(\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p-2}e\right)-\nabla\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}.e\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p-2}\right)dz

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{C}{\zeta^{*}}\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\bar{w}^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}+\mathcal{O}\left(\|\bar{w}\|_{L^{2p}(\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}\leq|z|\leq\zeta^{*})}^{2p}\right),

and

\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\bar{w}^{2p-1}f\bar{w}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\leq\frac{C}{\zeta^{*}}\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\bar{w}^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}.

Summing the four identities above proves the linear estimate (
221
).

Step 2

(Preliminary estimates)
. From the bound (
218
), we have the estimate

\displaystyle\left(\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\left(|h^{0}|+\zeta|\nabla h^{0}|\right)^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2p}}\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\log\nu|}\left(\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(\delta-\frac{1}{4})2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2p}}\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\log\nu|},

(222)

for
\delta
small enough. Similarly, using the bound (
217
), the estimate (
273
), and the bootstrap bounds (
126
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left(\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\left(|g.\nabla\Phi_{(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{2}})(\hat{w}^{0})}|+\zeta|\nabla(g.\nabla\Phi_{(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{2}})(\hat{w}^{0})})|\right)^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2p}}\leq C\|\hat{w}^{0}\|_{\textup{ex}}\nu^{2}\leq\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\log\nu|},

(223)

for
\tau_{0}
large enough. Similarly, we estimate from (
218
) for the nonradial part,

\left(\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\left(|h^{\perp}|+\zeta|\nabla h^{\perp}|\right)^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2p}}\lesssim\nu^{2}\left|\frac{x^{*}_{\tau}}{\mu}\right|\lesssim e^{-\kappa\tau},

(224)

and using the bound (
217
), the estimate (
273
), and the bootstrap bound (
130
):

\displaystyle\left(\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\left(|g.\nabla\Phi_{(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{2}})(\hat{w}^{\perp})}|+\zeta|\nabla(g.\nabla\Phi_{(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{2}})(\hat{w}^{\perp})})|\right)^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2p}}\lesssim\|\hat{w}^{\perp}\|_{\textup{ex}}\nu^{2}\leq e^{-\kappa\tau},

(225)

for
\tau_{0}
large enough.

Step 3

(The energy estimate for
\hat{w}^{0}
)
. We claim that there holds the energy estimate:

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\tau}\left(\frac{1}{2p}\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{0})^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(-\frac{\beta}{4}+\frac{C}{\zeta^{*}}\right)\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{0})^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}

(226)

\displaystyle+C\frac{K^{{}^{\prime}2p}\nu^{4p}}{|\log\nu|^{2p}}+C\left(\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{0})^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)^{\frac{2p-1}{2p}}\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\log\nu|}.

We compute from (
215
),

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\tau}\left(\frac{1}{2p}\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{0})^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)

(227)

\displaystyle=\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{0})^{2p-1}\left(-\beta\nabla.(z\hat{w}^{0})+\Delta\hat{w}^{0}+e.\nabla\hat{w}^{0}+f\hat{w}^{0}+g.\nabla\Phi_{(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}})\hat{w}^{0}}+h^{0}+N^{0}(\hat{w}^{\perp})\right)\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}.

For the last term there holds from (
201
) and (
130
),

\left(\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\left|N^{0}(\hat{w}^{\perp})\right|^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2p}}\lesssim\frac{e^{-\kappa\tau}}{\nu^{C}}\|\hat{w}^{\perp}\|_{\textup{ex}}\leq\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\log\nu|},

for
\tau_{0}
large enough. Hence, from Hölder, the above bound and the estimates (
223
), (
222
), we get

\displaystyle\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{0})^{2p-1}\left(g.\nabla\Phi_{(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}})\hat{w}^{0}}+h^{0}+N^{0}(\hat{w}^{\perp})\right)\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left(\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{0})^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)^{\frac{2p-1}{2p}}\times\left(\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\left|g.\nabla\Phi_{(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}})\hat{w}^{0}}+h^{0}+N^{0}(\hat{w}^{\perp})\right|^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2p}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\left(\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{0})^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)^{\frac{2p-1}{2p}}\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\log\nu|},

for
\tau_{0}
large enough. We inject the above identity in (
227
), and use the linear estimate (
221
) with the bounds (
124
) and (
128
) for the boundary terms and (
217
) for the potential and vector field, which yields (
226
).

Step 4

(The energy estimate for
\nabla(\hat{w}^{0})
)
. Let
\hat{w}^{i}=\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}\hat{w}
for
i=1,2
. We claim the energy estimate for
i=1,2
:

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\tau}\left(\frac{1}{2p}\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)\leq\left(-\frac{\beta}{4}+\frac{C}{\zeta^{*}}\right)\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}+\frac{C}{\zeta^{*}}\|\hat{w}^{0}\|_{\textup{ex}}^{2p}

(228)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad+C\frac{K^{{}^{\prime}2p}\nu^{4p}}{|\log\nu|^{2p}}+C\left(\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)^{\frac{2p-1}{2p}}\frac{K\nu^{2}}{|\log\nu|},

which we now prove. From (
215
) and the commutator relations
[\zeta\partial_{z_{i}},z.\nabla]=0
and
[\zeta\partial_{z_{i}},\Delta]=\zeta^{-2}\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}-2\zeta^{-2}z.\nabla(\zeta\partial_{z_{i}})
, we infer the evolution equation for
\hat{w}^{i}
:

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}\hat{w}^{i}=-\beta\nabla.(z\hat{w}^{i})+\Delta\hat{w}^{i}+\left(e-\frac{2z}{\zeta^{2}}\right).\nabla\hat{w}^{i}+\left(f+\zeta^{-2}-\frac{z.e}{\zeta^{2}}\right)\hat{w}^{i}+\mathcal{F},

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}=\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}e.\nabla\hat{w}^{0}+\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}f\hat{w}^{0}+\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}(g.\nabla\Phi_{(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{2}})\hat{w}^{0}})+\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}h^{0}+\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}N^{0}(\hat{w}^{\perp}),

giving the energy estimate with
e^{\prime}=e-2\zeta^{-2}z
and
f^{\prime}=f+\zeta^{-2}-\zeta^{-2}z.e
:

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\tau}\left(\frac{1}{2p}\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)

(229)

\displaystyle=\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p-1}\Big(-\beta\nabla.(z\hat{w}^{i})+\Delta\hat{w}^{i}+e^{\prime}.\nabla\hat{w}^{i}+f^{\prime}\hat{w}^{i}+\mathcal{F}\Big)\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}.

We apply for the linear part the estimate (
221
) with the bounds (
124
) and (
128
) for the boundary terms and (
217
) for the vector field
e^{\prime}
and the potential
f^{\prime}
:

\displaystyle\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p-1}\left(-\beta\nabla.(z\hat{w}^{i})+\Delta\hat{w}^{i}+e^{\prime}.\nabla\hat{w}^{i}+f^{\prime}\hat{w}^{i}\right)\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq-(2p-1)\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\bar{w}^{2p-2}|\nabla\bar{w}|^{2}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\left(-\frac{\beta}{4}+\frac{C}{\zeta^{*}}\right)\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}+C\frac{K^{{}^{\prime}2p}\nu^{4p}}{|\log\nu|^{2p}}.

We compute now the remaining terms. From Hölder, the bounds
|\zeta\nabla e|\lesssim 1
and
|\nabla f|\lesssim\zeta^{-2}
from (
217
), for any function
\bar{w}
:

\left(\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\left(|\partial_{z_{i}}e||\zeta\nabla\bar{w}|+|\zeta\bar{w}\partial_{z_{i}}f|\right)^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2p}}\lesssim\frac{1}{\zeta^{*}}\|\bar{w}\|_{\textup{ex}}.

(230)

Hence, using the above bound for
\bar{w}=\hat{w}^{0}
and Hölder:

\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p-1}\left(\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}e.\nabla\hat{w}^{0}+\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}f\hat{w}^{0}\right)\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\leq\frac{C}{\zeta^{*}}\|\hat{w}^{0}\|_{\textup{ex}}.

Again, from Hölder, the bounds (
223
) and (
222
)

\displaystyle\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p-1}\left(\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}(g.\nabla\Phi_{(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}})\hat{w}^{0}})+\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}h^{0}\right)\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)^{\frac{2p-1}{2p}}\left(\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\left|\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}(g.\nabla\Phi_{(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{2}})\hat{w}^{0}})+|\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}h^{0}|\right|^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2p}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\hat{w}^{0}\|_{\textup{ex}}^{2p-1}\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\log\nu|},

for
\nu
small enough, where we used (
273
) and (
126
). For the last term we integrate by parts, use Hölder and the bootstrap bounds in Definition
3.3
:

\displaystyle\left|\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p-1}\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}N^{0}(\hat{w}^{\perp})\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle\quad\leq C\frac{e^{-\kappa\tau}}{\nu}\left(\left(\frac{K^{\prime}\nu^{2}}{|\log\nu|}\right)^{2p}+\|\hat{w}^{0}\|_{\textup{ex}}^{2p-1}\|\hat{w}^{\perp}\|_{\textup{ex}}^{2p-1}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+C\frac{e^{-\kappa\tau}}{\nu}\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}|\nabla\hat{w}^{i}|^{2}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p-2}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}+C\frac{e^{-\kappa\tau}}{\nu}\|\hat{w}^{0}\|_{\textup{ex}}^{2p-1}\|\hat{w}^{\perp}\|_{\textup{ex}}^{2p-1}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C\frac{e^{-\kappa\tau}}{\nu}\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}|\nabla\hat{w}^{i}|^{2}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p-2}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}+C\left(\frac{K^{\prime}\nu^{2}}{|\log\nu|}\right)^{2p},

where we used Young inequality on the last line and took
\tau_{0}
large enough. The collection of above inequalities yields (
228
).

Step 5

(End of the proof for
\hat{w}^{0}
)
. We sum the identities (
226
) and (
228
) for
i=1,2
, concluding the proof of (
219
).

Step 6

(The energy estimate for
\hat{w}^{\perp}
)
. This step is very similar to Step 3 so we shall give less details. We claim that there holds the energy estimate

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\tau}\left(\frac{1}{2p}\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{\perp})^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)

(231)

\displaystyle\leq\left(-\frac{\beta}{4}+\frac{C}{\zeta^{*}}\right)\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{\perp})^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}+C\frac{K^{{}^{\prime}2p}\nu^{4p}}{|\log\nu|^{2p}}+C\left(\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{0})^{\perp}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)^{\frac{2p-1}{2p}}\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\log\nu|}.

We compute from (
216
),

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\tau}\left(\frac{1}{2p}\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{\perp})^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)

(232)

\displaystyle=\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{\perp})^{2p-1}\left(-\beta\nabla.(z\hat{w}^{\perp})+\Delta\hat{w}^{\perp}+e.\nabla\hat{w}^{\perp}+f\hat{w}^{\perp}+g.\nabla\Phi_{(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}})\hat{w}^{\perp}}+h^{\perp}+N^{\perp}(\hat{w}^{\perp})\right)\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}.

For the last term there holds from (
201
), (
137
) and (
138
),

\left(\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\left|N^{\perp}(\hat{w}^{\perp})\right|^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2p}}\lesssim\frac{e^{-\kappa\tau}}{\nu^{C}}\|\hat{w}^{\perp}\|_{\textup{ex}}\leq\frac{1}{\zeta^{*}}\|\hat{w}^{\perp}\|_{\textup{ex}},

for
\tau_{0}
large enough. Therefore from Hölder, the above bound and the bounds (
225
), (
224
):

\displaystyle\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{\perp})^{2p-1}\left(g.\nabla\Phi_{(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}})\hat{w}^{\perp}}+h^{\perp}+N^{\perp}(\hat{w}^{\perp})\right)\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\left(\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{\perp})^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)^{\frac{2p-1}{2p}}e^{-\kappa\tau}.

We inject the above identity into (
232
) and use the linear estimate (
221
) with the bounds (
128
) for the boundary terms and (
217
) for the potential and vector field to obtain (
231
).

Step 7

(The energy estimate for
\nabla(\hat{w}^{\perp})
)
. This step is very similar to Step 4. Let
\hat{w}^{i}=\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}\hat{w}^{\perp}
for
i=1,2
. We claim the energy estimate for
i=1,2
:

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\tau}\left(\frac{1}{2p}\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(-\frac{\beta}{4}+\frac{C}{\zeta^{*}}\right)\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}+\frac{C}{\zeta^{*}}\|\hat{w}^{0}\|_{\textup{ex}}^{2p}

(233)

\displaystyle+C\frac{K^{{}^{\prime}2p}\nu^{4p}}{|\log\nu|^{2p}}+C\left(\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)^{\frac{2p-1}{2p}}\frac{K\nu^{2}}{|\log\nu|}.

The evolution equation for
\hat{w}^{i}
is

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}\hat{w}^{i}

\displaystyle=-\beta\nabla.(z\hat{w}^{i})+\Delta\hat{w}^{i}+\left(e-\frac{2z}{\zeta^{2}}\right).\nabla\hat{w}^{i}+\left(f+\zeta^{-2}-\frac{z.e}{\zeta^{2}}\right)\hat{w}^{i}+H,

where

\displaystyle H=\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}e.\nabla\hat{w}^{0}+\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}f\hat{w}^{0}+\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}(g.\nabla\Phi_{(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{2}})\hat{w}^{\perp}})+\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}\big[h^{\perp}+N^{\perp}(\hat{w}^{\perp})\big].

Giving the energy estimate with
e^{\prime}=e-2\zeta^{-2}z
and
f^{\prime}=f+\zeta^{-2}-\zeta^{-2}z.e
, we write

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\tau}\left(\frac{1}{2p}\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p-1}\times\left(-\beta\nabla.(z\hat{w}^{i})+\Delta\hat{w}^{i}+e^{\prime}.\nabla\hat{w}^{i}+f^{\prime}\hat{w}^{i}+H\right)\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}.

We apply for the linear part the estimate (
221
) with the bounds (
124
) and (
128
) for the boundary terms and (
217
) for the vector field
e^{\prime}
and the potential
f^{\prime}
, and the bound (
230
) with
\bar{w}=w^{\perp}
:

\displaystyle\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p-1}\left(-\beta\nabla.(z\hat{w}^{i})+\Delta\hat{w}^{i}+e^{\prime}.\nabla\hat{w}^{i}+f^{\prime}\hat{w}^{i}+\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}e.\nabla\hat{w}^{\perp}+\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}f\hat{w}^{\perp}\right)\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq-(2p-1)\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\bar{w}^{2p-2}|\nabla w^{\perp}|^{2}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\left(-\frac{\beta}{4}+\frac{C}{\zeta^{*}}\right)\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}+CK^{{}^{\prime}2p}e^{-\kappa\tau}.

From Hölder and the bounds (
223
) and (
222
):

\displaystyle\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p-1}

\displaystyle\left(\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}(g.\nabla\Phi_{(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}})\hat{w}^{\perp}})+\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}h^{\perp}\right)\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}\leq C\|\hat{w}^{\perp}\|_{\textup{ex}}^{2p-1}e^{-\kappa\tau}.

We integrate by parts, use Hölder and the bootstrap bounds (
124
), (
128
), (
137
), (
138
),

\displaystyle\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p-1}\zeta\partial_{z_{i}}N^{\perp}(\hat{w}^{\perp})\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad=-\int(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p-1}N^{\perp}(\hat{w}^{\perp})\left(\partial_{z_{i}}\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p-1}+\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\partial_{z_{i}}\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p-1}\right)dz

\displaystyle\qquad-(2p-1)\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}\partial_{z_{i}}\hat{w}^{i}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p-2}\zeta N^{\perp}(\hat{w}^{\perp})\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C\frac{e^{-\kappa\tau}}{\nu}\left(\frac{K^{\prime}\nu^{2}}{|\log\nu|}\left(K^{\prime}e^{-\kappa\tau}\right)^{2p}+\frac{K^{\prime\prime}\nu^{2}}{|\log\nu|}\|\hat{w}\|_{\textup{ex}}^{2p-1}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}|\nabla\hat{w}^{i}|^{2}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p-2}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}+C\frac{e^{-\kappa\tau}}{\nu}\|\hat{w}\|_{\textup{ex}}^{2}\|\hat{w}^{\perp}\|_{\textup{ex}}^{2p-2}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\int\left(1-\chi_{\frac{\zeta^{*}}{4}}\right)\zeta^{(2-\frac{1}{4})2p}|\nabla\hat{w}^{i}|^{2}(\hat{w}^{i})^{2p-2}\frac{dz}{\zeta^{2}}+\left(K^{\prime}e^{-\kappa\tau}\right)^{2p},

for any where we used Young inequality on the last line and took
\tau_{0}
large enough. The collection of above inequalities yields (
233
). We sum the identities (
231
) and (
233
) for
i=1,2
, concluding the proof of (
220
).
∎

4.7. Proof of Proposition
3.4

We give the proof of Proposition (
3.4
) from which directly implies the conclusion of Theorem
1.1
. Assume that the solution is initially trapped in the sense of Definition
3.2
. We then define

\tau^{*}=\sup\left\{\tau_{1}\geq\tau_{0},\mbox{ such that the solution is trapped on }[\tau_{0},\tau_{1}]\right\}.

We assume by contradiction that
\tau_{1}<\infty
and will show that this is impossible by integrating in time the various modulation equations and Lyapunov functionals. Throughout the proof,
C
denotes a universal constant that is independent of the bootstrap constants
\kappa,N,K,K^{\prime},K^{\prime\prime}
and the dependence on
K,K^{\prime},K^{\prime\prime}
in the various
\mathcal{O}
’s is explicitly mentioned. Recall Remark
3.5
regarding the order in which the constants are chosen.

Step 1:

Improved modulation for
\nu
and
\beta
. We inject the identity (
148
) and the bootstrap bound (
123
) in (
147
), then use the eigenvalue expansion (
56
) and the compatibility condition (
119
) to get

\displaystyle\frac{\nu_{\tau}}{\nu}

\displaystyle=\beta-\beta\frac{a_{1}}{4\nu^{2}}(\tilde{\alpha}_{1}-1-\tilde{\alpha}_{0})+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{K\mathcal{D}(\tau)}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{3}}\right)

(234)

\displaystyle=\beta-\beta\left(\left(-1+\frac{1}{2\ln\nu}+\frac{\ln 2-\gamma-1-\ln\beta}{4|\ln\nu|^{2}}\right)\left(-1-\frac{1}{4|\ln\nu|^{2}}\right)\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{K\mathcal{D}(\tau)}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{3}}\right)

\displaystyle=\beta\left(\frac{1}{2\ln\nu}+\frac{\ln 2-\gamma-2-\ln\beta}{4|\ln\nu|^{2}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{K\mathcal{D}(\tau)}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{3}}\right).

We then inject this identity, the bootstrap bound (
123
) and the eigenvalue expansion (
56
) into (
148
) to obtain

\displaystyle a_{1,\tau}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\beta a_{1}\tilde{\alpha}_{1}-a_{1}\frac{\beta}{2|\ln\nu|^{2}}+\frac{a_{1}\beta_{\tau}}{\beta}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{3}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{K\mathcal{D}(\tau)}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta a_{1}\left(\frac{1}{\ln\nu}+\frac{\ln 2-\gamma-2-\ln\beta}{2|\ln\nu|^{2}}\right)+a_{1}\frac{\beta_{\tau}}{\beta}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{3}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{K\mathcal{D}(\tau)}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\right).

We differentiate the compatibility condition
-1+\frac{1}{2\ln\nu}+\frac{(\ln 2-\gamma-1-\ln\beta)}{4|\ln\nu|^{2}}=\frac{a_{1}}{4\nu^{2}}
and inject the two identities above to arrive at

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\beta_{\tau}}{\beta}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{3}}+\frac{K\mathcal{D}(\tau)}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\right)\right)\frac{a_{1}}{4\nu^{2}}=\left(\frac{a_{1,\tau}}{a_{1}}-2\frac{\nu_{\tau}}{\nu}\right)\frac{a_{1}}{4\nu^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\nu_{\tau}}{\nu}\left(-\frac{1}{2|\ln\nu|^{2}}-\frac{\ln 2-\gamma-1-\ln\beta}{2|\ln\nu|^{3}}\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{3}}+\frac{K\mathcal{D}(\tau)}{|\ln\nu|^{4}}\right).

Since
\frac{a_{1}}{4\nu^{2}}=-1+\mathcal{O}(|\ln\nu|^{-1})
and
\beta\sim 1/2
in the bootstrap, we obtain
\beta_{\tau}=O\left(\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{3}}+\frac{K\mathcal{D}(\tau)}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\right)
, from which and (
234
), we arrive at the system

\left\{\begin{array}[]{l l}\frac{\nu_{\tau}}{\nu}=\beta\left(\frac{1}{2\ln\nu}+\frac{\ln 2-\gamma-2-\ln\beta}{4|\ln\nu|^{2}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{K}{|\ln\nu|^{3}}\right),\\
\beta_{\tau}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{K}{|\ln\nu|^{3}}\right).\end{array}\right.

(235)

Step 2:

Reintegrating the modulation equations
. We introduce the renormalised time
\tilde{\tau}=2\beta_{0}\tau_{0}+2\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau}\beta
and write
\nu=\sqrt{2\beta^{-1}}e^{-\frac{2+\gamma}{2}}e^{-\sqrt{\tilde{\tau}/2}}\left(1+\nu^{\prime}\right)
. From (
235
), we have
\tilde{\tau}=2\beta\tau+O(\tau^{-1/2})
and

\frac{\nu_{\tau}}{\nu}=\frac{\nu^{\prime}_{\tau}}{1+\nu^{\prime}}-\frac{2\sqrt{2}\beta}{\sqrt{\tilde{\tau}}}+\mathcal{O}(|\beta_{\tau}|)=\frac{\nu^{\prime}_{\tau}}{1+\nu^{\prime}}-\frac{\beta}{\sqrt{2\tilde{\tau}}}+\mathcal{O}(\tau^{-3/2}).

From (
120
) and (
109
), one has
|\nu^{\prime}|\lesssim(\ln|\ln\nu|)|\ln\nu|^{-1}
and the linearisation provides

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\ln\nu}+\frac{\ln 2-\gamma-2-\ln\beta}{4|\ln\nu|^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\left(\ln(\nu^{\prime}+1)+\frac{\ln 2-\gamma-2-\ln\beta}{2}-\sqrt{\frac{\tilde{\tau}}{2}}\right)}+\frac{\ln 2-\gamma-2-\ln\beta}{2\tilde{\tau}+\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\tau})}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\tilde{\tau}}}-\frac{\nu^{\prime}}{\tilde{\tau}}+\mathcal{O}(\tau^{-\frac{3}{2}}).

Equation (
235
) is then transformed into

\nu^{\prime}_{\tau}=-\frac{\beta\nu^{\prime}}{\tilde{\tau}}+\mathcal{O}(K\tau^{-\frac{3}{2}})\quad\mbox{ so that }\quad\nu^{\prime}_{\tilde{\tau}}=-\frac{\nu^{\prime}}{2\tilde{\tau}}+\mathcal{O}(K\tilde{\tau}^{-\frac{3}{2}})

which reintegrated in time by using (
109
) gives

|\nu^{\prime}(\tau_{1})|=|\nu^{\prime}(\tilde{\tau}_{1})|\leq\frac{\tilde{\tau}_{0}}{\tilde{\tau}_{1}}|\nu^{\prime}(\tau_{0})|+\frac{CK\ln\tilde{\tau}_{1}}{\sqrt{\tilde{\tau}_{1}}}\leq\frac{K^{\prime}}{2}\frac{\ln|\ln\nu|}{|\ln\nu|}.

(236)

As
\beta_{\tau}=\mathcal{O}(K|\ln\nu|^{-3})=\mathcal{O}(K\tau^{-3/2})
, we use (
109
) to obtain

\left|\beta(\tau_{1})-\frac{1}{2}\right|=\left|\beta(\tau_{0})-\frac{1}{2}+\mathcal{O}(K\tau_{0}^{-1/2})\right|\leq CK\tau_{0}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\leq\frac{K^{\prime}}{2|\ln\nu_{0}|}.

(237)

Finally, we reintegrate the modulation equations (
149
) for the other parameters
a_{i}
for
i=2,...,n
. Using (
123
) and the fact that
\beta\geq 1/4
and
|\tilde{\alpha}_{i}|\leq 1/2
yield

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\tau}(a_{i}^{2})

\displaystyle=4\beta(1-n+\tilde{\alpha}_{n})a_{i}^{2}+2a_{i}\left(\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\mathcal{D}(\tau)}{|\ln\nu|}\|m_{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\right)\right)\leq-\frac{a_{i}^{2}}{2}+K\tau^{-2}e^{-2\sqrt{\beta\tau}}.

Reintegrating in time, using (
235
) and (
110
) yields that for some universal constant
C>0
the estimate

\displaystyle a_{i}^{2}(\tau_{1})

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\frac{\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}}{2}}a_{i}^{2}(\tau_{0})+e^{-\frac{\tau_{1}}{2}}\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau_{1}}e^{\frac{\tau}{2}}K\tau^{-2}e^{-2\sqrt{\beta\tau}}d\tau\leq C\tau_{1}^{-2}e^{-2\sqrt{\beta\tau_{1}}}+CK\tau_{1}^{-2}e^{-2\sqrt{\beta\tau_{1}}}

(238)

\displaystyle\leq CK\frac{\nu^{4}}{|\log\nu|^{4}}\leq\frac{K^{2}}{2}\frac{\nu^{4}}{|\log\nu|^{4}}.

Step 3:

The Lyapunov functionals
. We inject the estimate
|\mathcal{D}(\tau)|\leq C|\ln\nu|^{-1}
into (
147
) and (
148
) to obtain the estimate

|\mbox{Mod}_{0}|+|\mbox{Mod}_{1}|\leq C\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}.

We inject this estimate and the bootstrap bound (
123
) and the last estimate in (
238
) into (
169
) to get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{d\tau}\big\|\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}

\displaystyle\leq-2\beta\left(N-C\right)\big\|\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}+K\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\log\nu|^{3}}+CK\frac{\nu^{4}}{|\log\nu|^{2}}+C\frac{\nu^{4}}{|\log\nu|^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq-\big\|\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}+CK\frac{e^{-4\sqrt{\beta\tau}}}{\tau}

for
N
large enough. We integrate in time this identity and use the initial condition (
111
),

\displaystyle\big\|\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}(\tau_{1})\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}\leq e^{-2(\tau_{1}-\tau_{0})}\big\|\bar{m}_{\varepsilon}(\tau_{0})\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\frac{\bar{\omega}_{\nu}}{\zeta}}}+CKe^{-2\tau_{1}}\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau_{1}}e^{2\tau}\frac{e^{-4\sqrt{\beta\tau}}}{\tau}d\tau\leq CK\frac{e^{-4\sqrt{\beta\tau}}}{\tau}\leq\frac{K^{2}}{2}\frac{\nu^{4}}{|\log\nu|^{2}},

(239)

(where we used
|\beta_{\tau}|\lesssim K|\log\nu|^{3}
to integrate by parts in time). Next, we directly have from (
173
) the bound

\|m_{\varepsilon}(\tau_{1})\|_{H^{2}(\zeta_{*}\leq\zeta\leq\zeta^{*})}\leq\frac{K^{\prime}}{2}\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}.

(240)

We now turn to the inner estimates. Let
M\gg 1
be a large constant. Then for
\zeta_{*}
small enough, we obtain from (
191
) and the bootstrap bounds (
124
):

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\tau}\left[-e^{M\tau}\tilde{\nu}^{2}\int\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}\right]+e^{M\tau}\|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}}\leq CK^{{}^{\prime}2}e^{M\tau}\frac{\nu^{4}}{|\log\nu|^{2}}\leq CK^{{}^{\prime}2}e^{M\tau}\frac{e^{-4\sqrt{\beta\tau}}}{\tau},

from which and a reintegration in time and the dissipation estimate from (
113
), we derive

\displaystyle-\tilde{\nu}^{2}(\tau_{1})\int\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}(\tau_{1})\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}(\tau_{1})\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}+e^{-M\tau_{1}}\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau_{1}}e^{M\tau}\|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}}d\tau

\displaystyle\quad\leq-e^{-M(\tau_{1}-\tau_{0})}\tilde{\nu}^{2}(\tau_{0})\int\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}(\tau_{0})\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}(\tau_{0})\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}+e^{-M\tau_{1}}CK^{{}^{\prime}2}\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau_{1}}e^{M\tau}\frac{e^{-4\sqrt{\beta\tau}}}{\tau}d\tau

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{CK^{{}^{\prime}2}}{M}\frac{e^{-4\sqrt{\beta\tau}}}{\tau}\leq\frac{K^{{}^{\prime\prime}2}}{2M}\frac{\nu^{4}}{|\log\nu|^{2}}.

We inject the bound (
124
) into (
190
) to obtain

\frac{d}{d\tau}\left(e^{M\tau}\|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}}^{2}\right)\leq C\left(Me^{M\tau}\|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}}^{2}+\frac{K^{\prime}\nu^{4}}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}\right).

Reintegrating in time and using the previous dissipation estimate for
\|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}}^{2}
and (
113
) yield

\displaystyle\|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}(\tau_{1})\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}}^{2}\leq e^{-M(\tau_{1}-\tau_{0})}\|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}(\tau_{0})\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}}^{2}+CMe^{-M\tau_{1}}\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau_{1}}e^{M\tau}\|\mathscr{A}_{0}\tilde{m}_{v}^{*}\|_{L^{2}_{\frac{\omega_{0}}{\tilde{r}}}}^{2}d\tau+CK^{{}^{\prime}2}\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau_{1}}e^{M\tau}\frac{e^{-4\sqrt{\beta\tau}}}{\tau}d\tau

(241)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\leq\frac{CK^{{}^{\prime}2}e^{-4\sqrt{\beta\tau}}}{\tau}\leq\frac{K^{{}^{\prime\prime}2}}{2}\frac{\nu^{4}}{|\log\nu|^{2}}.

We turn to the estimates for the nonradial part. Reintegrating in time (
203
) directly gives the bound

\|\varepsilon^{\perp}\|_{0}\leq Ce^{-\kappa\tau}\leq\frac{K}{2}e^{-\kappa\tau}.

(242)

From the bootstrap bound (
128
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\tau}\left[\int\tilde{q}^{\perp}_{*}\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy-\int\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}^{\perp}_{*}\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy\right]\leq-\delta_{2}^{\prime}\left[\frac{1}{\zeta_{*}^{2}}\int\tilde{q}^{\perp}_{*}\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy-\int\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}^{\perp}_{*}\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}dy\right]+\frac{C(K^{2}+K^{{}^{\prime}2})}{\nu^{2}}e^{-2\kappa\tau}.

For
\kappa
small enough depending on
\delta_{2}^{\prime}
, we have from (
117
),

\displaystyle\left[\int\tilde{q}^{\perp}_{*}(\tau_{1})\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}(\tau_{1})dy-\int\mathscr{L}_{0}\tilde{q}^{\perp}_{*}(\tau_{1})\mathscr{M}\tilde{q}_{*}^{\perp}(\tau_{1})dy\right]\leq C(K^{2}+K^{{}^{\prime}2})\frac{e^{-2\kappa\tau}}{\nu^{2}}\leq\frac{K^{{}^{\prime\prime}2}}{2\nu^{2}}e^{-2\kappa\tau}.

(243)

Finally,(
219
) and (
220
) are directly reintegrated in time for
\zeta^{*}
large enough and
\kappa
small enough, using the initial bounds (
115
) and (
118
),

\|\hat{w}^{0}\|_{\textup{ex}}\leq\frac{K^{\prime\prime}\nu^{2}}{2|\ln\nu|},\quad\|\hat{w}^{\perp}\|_{\textup{ex}}\leq\frac{K^{\prime\prime}}{2}e^{-\kappa\tau}.

(244)

Step 4:

End of the proof of Proposition
3.4
. In Step 1 and Step 2, all the bounds involved in the Definition
3.3
have been improved by a factor
1/2
at time
\tau_{1}
, from (
236
), (
237
), (
238
), (
239
), (
240
), (
241
), (
242
), (
243
), (
244
). Hence, by a continuity argument, these bounds also hold true on some time interval
[\tau_{1},\tau_{1}+\delta]
for some small
\delta>0
. This contradicts the definition of
\tau_{1}
. Hence
\tau_{1}=\infty
and the solution is trapped in the regime
3.3
for
\tau\in[\tau_{0},+\infty)
. Knowing the solution is global in time
\tau
, reintegrating (
235
) yields

\beta(\tau)=\beta_{\infty}+\mathcal{O}(\tau^{-1/2}),\ \ \beta_{\infty}=\beta(\tau_{0})+\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\infty}\beta_{\tau}d\tau.

Recall the renormalised time
\tilde{\tau}
of Step 2, we obtain from the above identity that

\tau=\tilde{\tau}/2\beta_{\infty}+\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\tilde{\tau}}),

(245)

and since by definition
\mu_{\tau}/\mu=\beta
that
\mu(\tilde{\tau})=e^{-\tilde{\tau}/2}
. To go back to the original time variable, we integrate

\frac{d\tilde{\tau}}{dt}=\frac{d\tau}{dt}\frac{d\tilde{\tau}}{d\tau}=\frac{2}{\mu^{2}}\beta=2e^{\tilde{\tau}}\beta_{\infty}(1+\mathcal{O}(\tilde{\tau}^{-1/2})).

Solving the above equation, there exists
T>0
such that:

\tilde{\tau}=-\log\left(2\beta_{\infty}(T-t)\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{|\log\left((T-t)\right)|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right).

(246)

Hence, we obtain the following expression for the parabolic scale
\mu
,

\mu=e^{-\frac{\tilde{\tau}}{2}}=\sqrt{2\beta_{\infty}}\sqrt{T-t}\left(1+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{|\log\left((T-t)\right)|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)\right).

(247)

We get from
\beta=\beta_{\infty}+\mathcal{O}(\tau^{-1/2})=\beta_{\infty}+\mathcal{O}(|\ln(T-t)|^{-1/2})
and (
109
) that

\displaystyle\nu

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{2}{\beta}}e^{-\frac{2+\gamma}{2}}e^{-\sqrt{\frac{\tilde{\tau}}{2}}}\left(1+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)\right)=\sqrt{\frac{2}{\beta_{\infty}}}e^{-\frac{2+\gamma}{2}}e^{-\sqrt{\frac{|\ln(T-t)|}{2}}}\left(1+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{|\ln(T-t)|^{\frac{1}{4}}}\right)\right)

and hence, we get the desired blowup speed

\lambda=\mu\nu=2e^{-\frac{2+\gamma}{2}}\sqrt{T-t}e^{-\sqrt{\frac{|\ln(T-t)|}{2}}}\left(1+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{|\ln(T-t)|^{\frac{1}{4}}}\right)\right).

From (
201
) (the right hand side being less than
1
) and (
247
), we get the rough bound:

|x^{*}_{t}|=\frac{1}{\mu}\frac{|x^{*}_{\tau}|}{\mu}\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{T-t}}.

This implies that
x^{*}(t)
converges to some
X\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
as
t\rightarrow T
. We now turn to the proof of the continuity of the blowup time and blowup point with respect to the initial datum. Fix
u_{0}\in\mathcal{O}
with blowup time
T(u_{0})
, blowup point
x^{*}_{u}
with limit
X(u_{0})
, and renormalised time
\tau_{u}
, and
\delta>0
. From (
246
), (
245
) and the above inequality, for any
\tau_{1}
large enough, there exists
T(u_{0})-\delta/2\leq T_{1}<T(u_{0})
such that
\tau_{u}(T_{1})\geq\tau_{1}+1
and
|x^{*}_{u}(T)-X(u_{0})|\leq\delta/3
. By continuity we then obtain that for another solution
v
,
v
and all
v
-related parameters converge to
u
and
u
-related parameters on
[0,T_{1}]
as
v_{0}\rightarrow u_{0}
in
\mathcal{E}
. In particular,
\tau_{v}(T_{1})\geq\tau_{1}
and
|x^{*}_{v}(T_{1})-X(u_{0})|\leq\delta/2
. By (
246
), (
245
) and the above inequality, we get that for
\tau_{1}
large enough,
|X(v)-x^{*}_{v}(T_{1})|\leq\delta/2
and
|T(v_{0})-T_{1}|\leq\delta/2
. Hence by summing we obtain the desired continuity
|X(u_{0})-X(v_{0})|\leq\delta
and
|T(u_{0})-T(v_{0})|\leq\delta
. This concludes the proof of Proposition
3.4
as well as Theorem
1.1
.
\square

5. Unstable blowup dynamics

In this section, we sketch the idea to establish the existence of unstable blowup solutions to system (
1
) in the radial setting. The strategy of the proof of Theorem
1.1
has to be modified the following way. Fix
\ell\in\mathbb{N}
with
\ell\geq 2
and
N\in\mathbb{N}
with
N\gg 1
and consider the approximate solution of the form

m_{W}(\zeta,\tau)=Q_{\nu}(\zeta)+a_{\ell}(\tau)\big(\phi_{\ell,\nu}(\zeta)-\phi_{0,\nu}(\zeta)\big)+\sum_{n=1,n\neq\ell}^{N}a_{n}(\tau)\phi_{n,\nu}(\zeta)=Q_{\nu}+P_{\nu},

(248)

where the approximate perturbation is
P_{\nu}=P_{\ell,\nu}+P_{+,\nu}+P_{N,\nu}
with

P_{\ell,\nu}=a_{\ell}(\tau)\big(\phi_{\ell,\nu}(\zeta)-\phi_{0,\nu}(\zeta)\big),\;\;P_{+,\nu}=\sum_{i=1}^{\ell-1}a_{i}(\tau)\phi_{i,\nu}(\zeta),\;\;P_{N,\nu}=\sum_{i=\ell+1}^{N}a_{i}(\tau)\phi_{i,\nu}(\zeta)

and
\mathbf{a}(\tau)=(a_{1},\cdots,a_{N})(\tau)
and
\nu(\tau)
are unknown functions to be determined,
and
(\phi_{n,\nu})_{0\leq n\leq N}
are the eigenfunctions described in Proposition
2.1
, i.e,

\mathscr{A}^{\zeta}\phi_{n,\nu}(\zeta)=2\beta\Big(1-n+\frac{1}{2\ln\nu}+\bar{\alpha}_{n}(\nu)\Big)\phi_{n,\nu}(\zeta)

(249)

with
|\bar{\alpha}(\nu)|\lesssim\frac{1}{|\ln\nu|^{2}}
and
\phi_{n,\nu}
is given by (
61
). Here, the leading order term in the approximate perturbation is
P_{\ell,\nu}
which drives the law of blowup law. The first higher order term
P_{+,\nu}
contains
\ell-1
”unstable” directions for
\ell\geq 2
, that can be controlled by tuning the initial datum in a suitable way (see Definition
5.4
below) via a classical topological argument. The second higher order term
P_{N,\nu}
added to (
86
) is to get a large constant in the spectral gap (
60
) which is only used for the control of the solution and does not affect the leading dynamic of blowup

The generated error and derivation of unstable blowup rates.

The following lemma is similar to Lemma
3.1
, from which we can redesign the bootstrap regime
3.3
adapted for this case.

Lemma 5.1
.

Assume that
(\beta,\nu,\mathbf{a})
are
\mathcal{C}^{1}
maps
(\beta,\nu,\mathbf{a}):[\tau_{0},\tau_{1})\mapsto[1/2,2]\times(0,\nu^{*}]\times(0,a^{*}]^{\ell+1}
, for
0<\nu^{*},a^{*}\ll 1
and
1\ll\tau_{0}<\tau_{1}\leq+\infty
, with a priori bounds:

|\beta_{\tau}|\lesssim|\ln\nu|^{\frac{3}{2}},\quad\left|\frac{\nu_{\tau}}{\nu}\right|\lesssim 1,\quad|a_{\ell}|\lesssim\nu^{2},\quad|a_{n}|\lesssim\frac{\nu^{2}}{|\ln\nu|}\;\;\textup{for}\;\;1\leq n\neq\ell\leq N.

(250)

Then the error generated by (
248
) to the flow (
54
) can be decomposed as

\displaystyle m_{E}

\displaystyle=-\partial_{\tau}m_{W}+\partial_{\zeta}^{2}m_{W}-\frac{\partial_{\zeta}m_{W}}{\zeta}+\frac{\partial_{\zeta}(m_{W}^{2})}{2\zeta}-\beta\zeta\partial_{\zeta}m_{W}=\sum_{j=0}^{\ell}\textup{Mod}_{j}\times\phi_{j,\nu}+\tilde{m}_{E}+\frac{\partial_{\zeta}P_{\nu}^{2}}{2\zeta},

(251)

where

\displaystyle\textup{Mod}_{0}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\nu_{\tau}}{\nu}-\beta\right)8\nu^{2}+a_{\ell,\tau}-2\beta a_{\ell}\big(1+\frac{1}{2\ln\nu}+\bar{\alpha}_{0}\big),

\displaystyle\textup{Mod}_{j}

\displaystyle=-a_{j,\tau}+2\beta a_{j}\big(1-j+\frac{1}{2\ln\nu}+\bar{\alpha}_{j}\big)\quad\textup{for}\quad 1\leq j\leq N,

and
